Implement the following plan:

# Plano: bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php

## Context

O JetElements Download Button com attachment ID 90286 está em `/cultura/linha-do-tempo/` (blog 2), mas o attachment existe apenas no `wp_posts` (blog 1). O problema é sistêmico: **5 widgets JetElements + 2 handlers** sofrem do mesmo bug cross-blog por chamarem `wp_get_attachment_url()` e `get_attached_file()` sem context switching.

O `gallery-crossblog-fix.php` existente (v1.0.0) resolve parcialmente (apenas `wp_get_attachment_url` para gallery/lightbox do Elementor). A solução é **renomear + expandir** esse plugin em um único arquivo canônico com escopo genérico.

**Widgets JetElements afetados confirmados:**
- `jet-elements-audio.php` — `wp_get_attachment_url()` → áudios não carregam
- `jet-elements-video.php` — `wp_get_attachment_url()` → vídeos self-hosted não carregam
- `jet-elements-download-button.php` — `wp_get_attachment_url()` → nome do arquivo não exibido
- `class-jet-elements-download-handler.php` — `get_attached_file()` ×3 → tamanho não calculado + download falha
- `class-jet-elements-svg-manager.php` — `get_attached_file()` ×2 → dimensions SVG não calculadas

**O NML já cobre `wp_get_attachment_image_src()`** — não duplicar.

## Decisões de design

| Decisão | Escolha | Motivo |
|---|---|---|
| `try/finally` | **Não usar** | Padrão do projeto e do NML (referência): sequência linear com guards |
| `static $resolved/$failed` | **Usar** | Melhoria sobre NML e plugin atual — evita `switch_to_blog()` por ID já processado |
| Escopo do guard | `!== 2` | Site tem exatamente 2 blogs; hardcoded é correto e explícito |
| `wp_cache_set` timing | **Após `restore_current_blog()`** | Confirmado em `object-cache.php`: `blog_prefix` já voltou para 2, escrita vai ao namespace correto |
| `update_meta_cache` | **Dentro do guard de existência** | Evita query desnecessária ao banco do blog 1 para IDs inexistentes |

## Conteúdo completo do novo arquivo

```php
<?php
/**
 * Plugin Name:  BIT Cross-Blog Attachment Fix
 * Description:  Fixes cross-blog attachment resolution for blog 2 (/cultura/)
 *               accessing attachments stored in blog 1 (site principal).
 *               Covers: wp_get_attachment_url (lightbox, audio, video, download
 *               button), get_attached_file (download handler, SVG manager) e
 *               pré-população de cache para process_download e widget gallery.
 *               Complementa o Network Media Library, que já cobre
 *               wp_get_attachment_image_src().
 * Version:      1.0.0
 * Author:       Bureau IT
 * Network:      true
 */

if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) exit;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 1. Fix wp_get_attachment_url() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).
//    Cobre: lightbox do Elementor (add_lightbox_data_attributes), JetElements
//    audio, video e download button (exibição do nome do arquivo).
//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
add_filter( 'wp_get_attachment_url', function ( $url, $post_id ) {
	static $switched = false;
	static $resolved = [];
	static $failed   = [];

	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $url;
	if ( $url ) return $url;
	if ( isset( $failed[ $post_id ] ) ) return $url;
	if ( isset( $resolved[ $post_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $post_id ];
	if ( $switched ) return $url;

	$switched = true;
	switch_to_blog( 1 );
	$b1_url = wp_get_attachment_url( $post_id );
	restore_current_blog();
	$switched = false;

	if ( $b1_url ) {
		$resolved[ $post_id ] = $b1_url;
		return $b1_url;
	}

	$failed[ $post_id ] = true;
	return $url;
}, 999, 2 );

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 2. Fix get_attached_file() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).
//    Cobre: JetElements download handler (get_file_size + process_download) e
//    SVG manager (cálculo de dimensions).
//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
add_filter( 'get_attached_file', function ( $file, $attachment_id ) {
	static $switched = false;
	static $resolved = [];
	static $failed   = [];

	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $file;
	if ( $file ) return $file;
	if ( isset( $failed[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $file;
	if ( isset( $resolved[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $attachment_id ];
	if ( $switched ) return $file;

	$switched = true;
	switch_to_blog( 1 );
	$b1_file = get_attached_file( $attachment_id );
	restore_current_blog();
	$switched = false;

	if ( $b1_file ) {
		$resolved[ $attachment_id ] = $b1_file;
		return $b1_file;
	}

	$failed[ $attachment_id ] = true;
	return $file;
}, 999, 2 );

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 3. Pré-popula object cache do blog 2 com post + meta do blog 1 antes que
//    JetElements process_download() (init priority 99) chame get_post($id).
//    Sem isso, get_post() retorna null → Fatal Error PHP 8.
//    Hash estrutura: [ 'sha1hash' => post_id ] (gravada no blog 2 pelo widget).
//    update_meta_cache() chamado somente se o post existir no blog 1.
//    wp_cache_set() após restore_current_blog() — escreve no namespace do blog 2.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
add_action( 'init', function () {
	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;
	if ( empty( $_GET['jet_download'] ) ) return;

	// Resolve hash → ID no blog 2 (onde a option foi gravada pelo widget)
	$hashes = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );
	$hash   = sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_GET['jet_download'] ) );
	$id     = isset( $hashes[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes[ $hash ] : 0;

	// Fallback: tenta ler do blog 1 (edge case: hash gerado no admin do blog 1)
	if ( ! $id ) {
		switch_to_blog( 1 );
		$hashes_b1 = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );
		restore_current_blog();
		$id = isset( $hashes_b1[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes_b1[ $hash ] : 0;
	}

	if ( ! $id ) return;

	// Busca post do blog 1; warm de meta somente se existir (evita query desnecessária)
	switch_to_blog( 1 );
	$post = get_post( $id );
	$meta = false;
	if ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {
		update_meta_cache( 'post', [ $id ] );
		$meta = wp_cache_get( $id, 'post_meta' );
	}
	restore_current_blog();

	// wp_cache_set aqui usa blog_prefix=2 (restore_current_blog já executou)
	if ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {
		wp_cache_set( $id, $post, 'posts' );
		if ( $meta ) wp_cache_set( $id, $meta, 'post_meta' );
	}
}, 5 );

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 4. Antes do render do widget gallery do Elementor, pré-popula o object cache
//    e postmeta cache (namespace blog 2) com dados de attachments do blog 1.
//    Garante que get_post($id) e get_post_meta($id, '_wp_attachment_image_alt')
//    retornem dados corretos para IDs do blog 1 dentro do render da gallery.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
add_action( 'elementor/widget/before_render_content', function ( $widget ) {
	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;
	if ( $widget->get_name() !== 'gallery' ) return;

	$settings = $widget->get_settings_for_display();
	$ids = [];
	foreach ( $settings['galleries'] ?? [] as $gallery ) {
		foreach ( $gallery['multiple_gallery'] ?? [] as $item ) {
			$id = absint( $item['id'] ?? 0 );
			if ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;
		}
	}
	if ( empty( $ids ) ) return;

	// IDs ausentes ou com tipo errado em blog 2
	$missing = [];
	foreach ( $ids as $id ) {
		$post = get_post( $id );
		if ( ! $post || $post->post_type !== 'attachment' ) {
			$missing[] = $id;
		}
	}
	if ( empty( $missing ) ) return;

	// Busca posts e meta do blog 1 (batch — uma única switch_to_blog por render)
	switch_to_blog( 1 );
	update_meta_cache( 'post', $missing );
	$b1_posts = [];
	$b1_metas = [];
	foreach ( $missing as $id ) {
		$post = get_post( $id );
		if ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {
			$b1_posts[ $id ] = $post;
			$b1_metas[ $id ] = wp_cache_get( $id, 'post_meta' );
		}
	}
	restore_current_blog();

	// Popula cache do blog 2 (blog_prefix=2 após restore)
	foreach ( $b1_posts as $id => $post ) {
		wp_cache_set( $id, $post, 'posts' );
		if ( ! empty( $b1_metas[ $id ] ) ) {
			wp_cache_set( $id, $b1_metas[ $id ], 'post_meta' );
		}
	}
}, 5, 1 );
```

     1→<?php
     2→/**
     3→ * Plugin Name:  Gallery Attachment Title Fix
     4→ * Description:  In blog 2 context, when get_the_title() is called for an ID that
     5→ *               doesn't exist or is a revision (not an attachment), falls back to
     6→ *               blog 1's attachment title. Fixes Elementor gallery lightbox captions
     7→ *               for Miguel Penha's gallery on /cultura/galeria/.
     8→ * Version:      1.0.0
     9→ * Author:       Bureau IT
    10→ * Network:      true
    11→ */

    13→if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) exit;
    14→
    15→add_filter( 'the_title', function ( $title, $post_id ) {
    16→    // Only apply in blog 2 context
    17→    if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) {
    18→        return $title;
    19→    }
    20→
    21→    $post = get_post( $post_id );
    22→
    23→    // Only intervene when post doesn't exist in blog 2 or is not an attachment
    24→    if ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {
    25→        return $title;
    26→    }
    27→
    28→    // Cache blog 1 attachment title lookups to avoid repeated switch_to_blog() calls
    29→    static $cache = [];
    30→
    31→    if ( ! array_key_exists( $post_id, $cache ) ) {
    32→        switch_to_blog( 1 );
    33→        $b1 = get_post( $post_id );
    34→        restore_current_blog();
    35→        $cache[ $post_id ] = ( $b1 && $b1->post_type === 'attachment' )
    36→            ? $b1->post_title
    37→            : null;
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    return $cache[ $post_id ] !== null ? $cache[ $post_id ] : $title;
    41→}, 10, 2 );

Fix get_attached_file() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).\n//    Cobre: JetElements download handler (get_file_size + process_download) e\n//    SVG manager (cálculo de dimensions).\n//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'get_attached_file', function ( $file, $attachment_id ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $file;\n\tif ( $file ) return $file;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $file;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $attachment_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $file;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t$b1_file = get_attached_file( $attachment_id );\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_file ) {\n\t\t$resolved[ $attachment_id ] = $b1_file;\n\t\treturn $b1_file;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $attachment_id ] = true;\n\treturn $file;\n}, 999, 2 );\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 3. Pré-popula object cache do blog 2 com post + meta do blog 1 antes que\n//    JetElements process_download() (init priority 99) chame get_post($id).\n//    Sem isso, get_post() retorna null → Fatal Error PHP 8.\n//    Hash estrutura: [ 'sha1hash' => post_id ] (gravada no blog 2 pelo widget).\n//    update_meta_cache() chamado somente se o post existir no blog 1.\n//    wp_cache_set() após restore_current_blog() — escreve no namespace do blog 2.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_action( 'init', function () {\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;\n\tif ( empty( $_GET['jet_download'] ) ) return;\n\n\t// Resolve hash → ID no blog 2 (onde a option foi gravada pelo widget)\n\t$hashes = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );\n\t$hash   = sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_GET['jet_download'] ) );\n\t$id     = isset( $hashes[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes[ $hash ] : 0;\n\n\t// Fallback: tenta ler do blog 1 (edge case: hash gerado no admin do blog 1)\n\tif ( ! $id ) {\n\t\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t\t$hashes_b1 = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );\n\t\trestore_current_blog();\n\t\t$id = isset( $hashes_b1[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes_b1[ $hash ] : 0;\n\t}\n\n\tif ( ! $id ) return;\n\n\t// Busca post do blog 1; warm de meta somente se existir (evita query desnecessária)\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t$post = get_post( $id );\n\t$meta = false;\n\tif ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {\n\t\tupdate_meta_cache( 'post', [ $id ] );\n\t\t$meta = wp_cache_get( $id, 'post_meta' );\n\t}\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\n\t// wp_cache_set aqui usa blog_prefix=2 (restore_current_blog já executou)\n\tif ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {\n\t\twp_cache_set( $id, $post, 'posts' );\n\t\tif ( $meta ) wp_cache_set( $id, $meta, 'post_meta' );\n\t}\n}, 5 );\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 4. Antes do render do widget gallery do Elementor, pré-popula o object cache\n//    e postmeta cache (namespace blog 2) com dados de attachments do blog 1.\n//    Garante que get_post($id) e get_post_meta($id, '_wp_attachment_image_alt')\n//    retornem dados corretos para IDs do blog 1 dentro do render da gallery.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_action( 'elementor/widget/before_render_content', function ( $widget ) {\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;\n\tif ( $widget->get_name() !== 'gallery' ) return;\n\n\t$settings = $widget->get_settings_for_display();\n\t$ids = [];\n\tforeach ( $settings['galleries'] ?? [] as $gallery ) {\n\t\tforeach ( $gallery['multiple_gallery'] ?? [] as $item ) {\n\t\t\t$id = absint( $item['id'] ?? 0 );\n\t\t\tif ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif ( empty( $ids ) ) return;\n\n\t// IDs ausentes ou com tipo errado em blog 2\n\t$missing = [];\n\tforeach ( $ids as $id ) {\n\t\t$post = get_post( $id );\n\t\tif ( ! $post || $post->post_type !== 'attachment' ) {\n\t\t\t$missing[] = $id;\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif ( empty( $missing ) ) return;\n\n\t// Busca posts e meta do blog 1 (batch — uma única switch_to_blog por render)\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\tupdate_meta_cache( 'post', $missing );\n\t$b1_posts = [];\n\t$b1_metas = [];\n\tforeach ( $missing as $id ) {\n\t\t$post = get_post( $id );\n\t\tif ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {\n\t\t\t$b1_posts[ $id ] = $post;\n\t\t\t$b1_metas[ $id ] = wp_cache_get( $id, 'post_meta' );\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\n\t// Popula cache do blog 2 (blog_prefix=2 após restore)\n\tforeach ( $b1_posts as $id => $post ) {\n\t\twp_cache_set( $id, $post, 'posts' );\n\t\tif ( ! empty( $b1_metas[ $id ] ) ) {\n\t\t\twp_cache_set( $id, $b1_metas[ $id ], 'post_meta' );\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}, 5, 1 );\n"
}

> AGENT

The problem is clear: WordPress core's `wp_get_attachment_url()` calls `get_post()` first and returns `false` immediately (without applying the filter) when the post is null. Let me check the WP source to confirm:

```
function wp_get_attachment_url( $attachment_id = 0 ) {
	global $pagenow;

	$attachment_id = (int) $attachment_id;

	$post = get_post( $attachment_id );

	if ( ! $post ) {
		return false;
	}

	if ( 'attachment' !== $post->post_type ) {
		return false;
	}

	$url = '';
	// Get attached file.
	$file = get_post_meta( $post->ID, '_wp_attached_file', true );
	if ( $file ) {
		// Get upload directory.
		$uploads = wp_get_upload_dir();
		if ( $uploads && false === $uploads['error'] ) {
			// Check that the upload base exists in the file location.
			if ( str_starts_with( $file, $uploads['basedir'] ) ) {
				// Replace file location with url location.
				$url = str_replace( $uploads['basedir'], $uploads['baseurl'], $file );
```

> AGENT

Confirmed — `wp_get_attachment_url()` returns `false` before applying the filter when `get_post()` returns null. The filter at priority 999 is never reached. The fix needs to intercept `get_post()` instead, or use a different approach.

The `gallery-crossblog-fix.php` had the same problem but worked for gallery because the gallery's cache pre-population (hook 4) makes `get_post()` return the correct object before the gallery renders. For the download button / JetElements widgets, hook 3 handles `init` — but for `wp_get_attachment_url` in general, we need to pre-populate the post cache too.

The correct fix: pre-populate `wp_cache_set` for cross-blog posts so `get_post()` returns them in blog 2 context. Let me update the plugin:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01CNcFJs4a2dCwfpzdqA2E8e
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php",
  "old_string": "// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 1. Fix wp_get_attachment_url() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).\n//    Cobre: lightbox do Elementor (add_lightbox_data_attributes), JetElements\n//    audio, video e download button (exibição do nome do arquivo).\n//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'wp_get_attachment_url', function ( $url, $post_id ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $url;\n\tif ( $url ) return $url;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $post_id ] ) ) return $url;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $post_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $post_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $url;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t$b1_url = wp_get_attachment_url( $post_id );\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_url ) {\n\t\t$resolved[ $post_id ] = $b1_url;\n\t\treturn $b1_url;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $post_id ] = true;\n\treturn $url;\n}, 999, 2 );",
  "new_string": "// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 1. Fix wp_get_attachment_url() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).\n//    Cobre: lightbox do Elementor (add_lightbox_data_attributes), JetElements\n//    audio, video e download button (exibição do nome do arquivo).\n//\n//    Problema: wp_get_attachment_url() retorna false antes de aplicar o filtro\n//    quando get_post($id) retorna null (post não existe no blog 2). A solução é\n//    interceptar get_post() via filtro 'get_post' para pré-popular o objeto no\n//    namespace do blog 2 antes que wp_get_attachment_url() verifique o post.\n//\n//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'get_post', function ( $post, $post_id, $filter ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $post;\n\tif ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) return $post;\n\tif ( ! $post_id ) return $post;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $post_id ] ) ) return $post;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $post_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $post_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $post;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t$b1_post = get_post( $post_id );\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_post && $b1_post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {\n\t\t// Popula cache no namespace do blog 2 para evitar re-fetch\n\t\twp_cache_set( $post_id, $b1_post, 'posts' );\n\t\t$resolved[ $post_id ] = $b1_post;\n\t\treturn $b1_post;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $post_id ] = true;\n\treturn $post;\n}, 999, 3 );"
}

```
function get_post( $post = null, $output = OBJECT, $filter = 'raw' ) {
	if ( empty( $post ) && isset( $GLOBALS['post'] ) ) {
		$post = $GLOBALS['post'];
	}

	if ( $post instanceof WP_Post ) {
		$_post = $post;
	} elseif ( is_object( $post ) ) {
		if ( empty( $post->filter ) ) {
			$_post = sanitize_post( $post, 'raw' );
			$_post = new WP_Post( $_post );
		} elseif ( 'raw' === $post->filter ) {
			$_post = new WP_Post( $post );
		} else {
			$_post = WP_Post::get_instance( $post->ID );
		}
	} else {
		$_post = WP_Post::get_instance( $post );
	}

	if ( ! $_post ) {
		return null;
	}

	$_post = $_post->filter( $filter );

	if ( ARRAY_A === $output ) {
		return $_post->to_array();
	} elseif ( ARRAY_N === $output ) {
		return array_values( $_post->to_array() );
	}

	return $_post;
}

	public static function get_instance( $post_id ) {
		global $wpdb;

		$post_id = (int) $post_id;
		if ( $post_id <= 0 ) {
			return false;
		}

		$_post = wp_cache_get( $post_id, 'posts' );

		if ( ! $_post ) {
			$_post = $wpdb->get_row( $wpdb->prepare( "SELECT * FROM $wpdb->posts WHERE ID = %d LIMIT 1", $post_id ) );

			if ( ! $_post ) {
				return false;
			}

			$_post = sanitize_post( $_post, 'raw' );
			wp_cache_add( $_post->ID, $_post, 'posts' );
		} elseif ( empty( $_post->filter ) || 'raw' !== $_post->filter ) {
			$_post = sanitize_post( $_post, 'raw' );
		}

		return new WP_Post( $_post );
	}

> AGENT

`WP_Post::get_instance()` checks `wp_cache_get($post_id, 'posts')` then queries `$wpdb->posts` — which in blog 2 context is `wp_2_posts`, where the attachment doesn't exist. 

The solution is to pre-populate the object cache before the call. The `get_post` filter doesn't exist in WordPress. Instead, we need to use `wp_cache_set` to populate `posts` cache with the blog 1 attachment data **in the blog 2 namespace** before `wp_get_attachment_url` is called.

The correct approach is to hook into the WP object cache directly. Since there's no filter on `get_post()` itself, we need to pre-populate the cache proactively. The gallery widget pre-populates in `before_render_content` (hook 4) — for JetElements widgets we need a similar approach.

Let me reconsider: the `wp_get_attachment_url` filter IS applied at line 7027, but only when `$post` is not null. Looking at the code flow: `get_post()` → `WP_Post::get_instance()` → `wp_cache_get('posts')` → if miss, queries `$wpdb->posts`. 

The real fix is to pre-populate `wp_cache_set($id, $b1_post, 'posts')` in the blog 2 namespace. We can do this via `posts_results` or `the_posts` filter, or by intercepting the DB query. 

Actually the cleanest approach: hook into `pre_get_posts` is wrong too. The real hook is `posts_pre_query` or intercepting at WP_Query level.

Wait — the simplest fix: **pre-populate the Redis/object cache when any request on blog 2 is made**. We can use a `wp_cache_get` miss hook... but there isn't one.

The most practical solution: use the existing `wp_get_attachment_url` filter but ensure the `$url` parameter is `false`/empty string when it reaches us. Looking at the code again:

```php
$url = apply_filters( 'wp_get_attachment_url', $url, $post->ID );
```

This only runs when `$post` is not null. So when the post doesn't exist in blog 2, the filter is never called.

**Alternative approach:** Intercept `$wpdb` query via `query` filter to redirect cross-blog post lookups. Or better: use `pre_option` / existing WP hooks.

The cleanest solution that works: **override `wp_cache_get` behavior by pre-populating it on `init`**. But we can't intercept `wp_cache_get`.

The real solution used by NML: they override `wp_get_attachment_image_src` filter which IS called even for cross-blog (because it's applied by Elementor after its own logic). For `wp_get_attachment_url`, we need a different strategy.

**Best approach**: Hook `jetpack_posts` or intercept at JetElements widget render level. Or: use the `the_post` action / WP_Query filter to detect cross-blog lookups.

Actually the simplest working fix: **populate `wp_cache_set` on `wp_loaded` for all attachment IDs that JetElements widgets reference**. But we'd need to know the IDs in advance.

add_filter( 'wp_get_attachment_image_src', function( $image, $attachment_id, $size, bool $icon ) {
	static $switched = false;

	if ( $switched ) {
		return $image;
	}

	if ( is_media_site() ) {
		return $image;
	}

	switch_to_media_site();

	$switched = true;
	$image    = wp_get_attachment_image_src( $attachment_id, $size, $icon );
	$switched = false;

	restore_current_blog();

	return $image;
}, 999, 4 );

/**
 * Filters the default gallery shortcode output so it shows media from the network media library site.
 *
 * @param string $output   The gallery output.
 * @param array  $attr     Attributes of the gallery shortcode.
 * @param int    $instance Unique numeric ID of this gallery shortcode instance.
 * @return string The gallery output.
 */
function filter_post_gallery( string $output, array $attr, int $instance ) : string {
	remove_filter( 'post_gallery', __NAMESPACE__ . '\filter_post_gallery', 0 );

	switch_to_media_site();
	$output = gallery_shortcode( $attr );
	restore_current_blog();

	add_filter( 'post_gallery', __NAMESPACE__ . '\filter_post_gallery', 0, 3 );

	return $output;
```

> AGENT

NML's `wp_get_attachment_image_src` filter works because `wp_get_attachment_image_src()` **does** apply the filter even when the image is not found (it passes `false` as `$image`). But `wp_get_attachment_url()` returns before the filter when post is null.

The solution: **intercept at a lower level**. We need to use `posts_results` filter on WP_Query, or intercept the `get_post()` call before it queries the DB by pre-loading the cache.

Looking at `WP_Post::get_instance()`: it calls `wp_cache_get($post_id, 'posts')` first. If we pre-populate this cache in the blog 2 namespace, `get_post()` will return the blog 1 attachment.

The key insight: we need a hook that fires **before `wp_get_attachment_url()` is called** but after we know the attachment ID. For JetElements widgets, that's `elementor/widget/before_render_content`. For generic cases, we need a different approach.

**The correct hook**: Use `wp_cache_get_last_changed` or better — hook into `query` filter on `$wpdb` to intercept the SELECT on `wp_2_posts` for attachment IDs and return blog 1 data.

     1→<?php
     2→/**
     3→ * Plugin Name:  BIT Cross-Blog Attachment Fix
     4→ * Description:  Fixes cross-blog attachment resolution for blog 2 (/cultura/)
     5→ *               accessing attachments stored in blog 1 (site principal).
     6→ *               Covers: wp_get_attachment_url (lightbox, audio, video, download
     7→ *               button), get_attached_file (download handler, SVG manager) e
     8→ *               pré-população de cache para process_download e widget gallery.
     9→ *               Complementa o Network Media Library, que já cobre
    10→ *               wp_get_attachment_image_src().
    11→ * Version:      1.0.0
    12→ * Author:       Bureau IT
    13→ * Network:      true
    14→ */
    15→
    16→if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) exit;
    17→
    18→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    19→// 1. Fix wp_get_attachment_url() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).
    20→//    Cobre: lightbox do Elementor (add_lightbox_data_attributes), JetElements
    21→//    audio, video e download button (exibição do nome do arquivo).
    22→//
    23→//    Problema: wp_get_attachment_url() retorna false antes de aplicar o filtro
    24→//    quando get_post($id) retorna null (post não existe no blog 2). A solução é
    25→//    interceptar get_post() via filtro 'get_post' para pré-popular o objeto no
    26→//    namespace do blog 2 antes que wp_get_attachment_url() verifique o post.
    27→//
    28→//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.
    29→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    30→add_filter( 'get_post', function ( $post, $post_id, $filter ) {
    31→	static $switched = false;
    32→	static $resolved = [];
    33→	static $failed   = [];
    34→
    35→	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $post;
    36→	if ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) return $post;
    37→	if ( ! $post_id ) return $post;
    38→	if ( isset( $failed[ $post_id ] ) ) return $post;
    39→	if ( isset( $resolved[ $post_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $post_id ];
    40→	if ( $switched ) return $post;
    41→
    42→	$switched = true;
    43→	switch_to_blog( 1 );
    44→	$b1_post = get_post( $post_id );
    45→	restore_current_blog();
    46→	$switched = false;
    47→
    48→	if ( $b1_post && $b1_post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {
    49→		// Popula cache no namespace do blog 2 para evitar re-fetch
    50→		wp_cache_set( $post_id, $b1_post, 'posts' );
    51→		$resolved[ $post_id ] = $b1_post;
    52→		return $b1_post;
    53→	}
    54→
    55→	$failed[ $post_id ] = true;
    56→	return $post;
    57→}, 999, 3 );
    58→
    59→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    60→// 2. Fix get_attached_file() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).
    61→//    Cobre: JetElements download handler (get_file_size + process_download) e
    62→//    SVG manager (cálculo de dimensions).
    63→//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.
    64→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    65→add_filter( 'get_attached_file', function ( $file, $attachment_id ) {
    66→	static $switched = false;
    67→	static $resolved = [];
    68→	static $failed   = [];
    69→
    70→	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $file;
    71→	if ( $file ) return $file;

> AGENT

I see the `get_post` filter doesn't exist in WordPress core — `get_post()` doesn't have a filter. The filter check confirmed `has_filter("wp_get_attachment_url")` returns `true` but `get_post` isn't a real filter.

The correct strategy: since `WP_Post::get_instance()` first checks `wp_cache_get($post_id, 'posts')`, the fix needs to **pre-populate that cache in blog 2 namespace** before the URL function is called. 

For JetElements widgets, the hook `elementor/widget/before_render_content` is already doing this (hook 4 for gallery). We need to add similar pre-population for JetElements widgets (audio, video, download button).

Additionally, for `get_attached_file()`, that hook **does** work because `get_attached_file()` applies the filter regardless. Let me verify:

  "content": "<?php\n/**\n * Plugin Name:  BIT Cross-Blog Attachment Fix\n * Description:  Fixes cross-blog attachment resolution for blog 2 (/cultura/)\n *               accessing attachments stored in blog 1 (site principal).\n *               Covers: wp_get_attachment_url (lightbox, audio, video, download\n *               button), get_attached_file (download handler, SVG manager) e\n *               pré-população de cache para process_download e widget gallery.\n *               Complementa o Network Media Library, que já cobre\n *               wp_get_attachment_image_src().\n * Version:      1.0.0\n * Author:       Bureau IT\n * Network:      true\n */\n\nif ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) exit;\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// HELPER: pré-popula object cache + postmeta do blog 2 com dados do blog 1\n// para uma lista de attachment IDs. Uma única switch_to_blog() por chamada.\n// Usado pelos hooks de widget antes de renderizar (hooks 3 e 4).\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nfunction bit_crossblog_warm_cache( array $ids ) : void {\n\tstatic $warmed = [];\n\n\t$missing = array_filter( $ids, function ( $id ) use ( &$warmed ) {\n\t\tif ( isset( $warmed[ $id ] ) ) return false;\n\t\t$post = wp_cache_get( $id, 'posts' );\n\t\treturn ! $post || $post->post_type !== 'attachment';\n\t} );\n\n\tif ( empty( $missing ) ) return;\n\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\tupdate_meta_cache( 'post', $missing );\n\t$b1_posts = [];\n\t$b1_metas = [];\n\tforeach ( $missing as $id ) {\n\t\t$post = get_post( $id );\n\t\tif ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {\n\t\t\t$b1_posts[ $id ] = $post;\n\t\t\t$b1_metas[ $id ] = wp_cache_get( $id, 'post_meta' );\n\t\t}\n\t\t$warmed[ $id ] = true;\n\t}\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\n\t// wp_cache_set após restore — blog_prefix=2, escreve no namespace correto\n\tforeach ( $b1_posts as $id => $post ) {\n\t\twp_cache_set( $id, $post, 'posts' );\n\t\tif ( ! empty( $b1_metas[ $id ] ) ) {\n\t\t\twp_cache_set( $id, $b1_metas[ $id ], 'post_meta' );\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 1. Fix wp_get_attachment_url() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).\n//    Cobre: lightbox do Elementor (add_lightbox_data_attributes), JetElements\n//    audio, video e download button (exibição do nome do arquivo).\n//\n//    Nota: wp_get_attachment_url() só aplica este filtro quando $post != null.\n//    Para IDs cross-blog, o cache é pré-populado pelo hook de widget (hook 3/4)\n//    antes do render. Este filtro cobre o fallback para lightbox do Elementor\n//    e qualquer chamada fora do contexto de widget render.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'wp_get_attachment_url', function ( $url, $post_id ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $url;\n\tif ( $url ) return $url;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $post_id ] ) ) return $url;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $post_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $post_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $url;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t$b1_url = wp_get_attachment_url( $post_id );\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_url ) {\n\t\t$resolved[ $post_id ] = $b1_url;\n\t\treturn $b1_url;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $post_id ] = true;\n\treturn $url;\n}, 999, 2 );\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 2. Fix get_attached_file() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).\n//    Cobre: JetElements download handler (get_file_size + process_download) e\n//    SVG manager (cálculo de dimensions).\n//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'get_attached_file', function ( $file, $attachment_id ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $file;\n\tif ( $file ) return $file;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $file;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $attachment_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $file;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t$b1_file = get_attached_file( $attachment_id );\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_file ) {\n\t\t$resolved[ $attachment_id ] = $b1_file;\n\t\treturn $b1_file;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $attachment_id ] = true;\n\treturn $file;\n}, 999, 2 );\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 3. Pré-popula object cache do blog 2 com post + meta do blog 1 antes que\n//    JetElements process_download() (init priority 99) chame get_post($id).\n//    Sem isso, get_post() retorna null → Fatal Error PHP 8.\n//    Hash estrutura: [ 'sha1hash' => post_id ] (gravada no blog 2 pelo widget).\n//    update_meta_cache() chamado somente se o post existir no blog 1.\n//    wp_cache_set() após restore_current_blog() — escreve no namespace do blog 2.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_action( 'init', function () {\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;\n\tif ( empty( $_GET['jet_download'] ) ) return;\n\n\t// Resolve hash → ID no blog 2 (onde a option foi gravada pelo widget)\n\t$hashes = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );\n\t$hash   = sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_GET['jet_download'] ) );\n\t$id     = isset( $hashes[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes[ $hash ] : 0;\n\n\t// Fallback: tenta ler do blog 1 (edge case: hash gerado no admin do blog 1)\n\tif ( ! $id ) {\n\t\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t\t$hashes_b1 = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );\n\t\trestore_current_blog();\n\t\t$id = isset( $hashes_b1[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes_b1[ $hash ] : 0;\n\t}\n\n\tif ( ! $id ) return;\n\n\tbit_crossblog_warm_cache( [ $id ] );\n}, 5 );\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 4. Antes do render de widgets Elementor/JetElements, pré-popula o object cache\n//    (namespace blog 2) com posts + meta de attachments do blog 1.\n//    Garante que get_post($id) retorne correto → wp_get_attachment_url() funciona.\n//\n//    Widgets cobertos:\n//    - gallery (Elementor): IDs em settings.galleries[].multiple_gallery[].id\n//    - jet-audio: ID em settings.audio_source_url.id\n//    - jet-video: ID em settings.video_sources[].source_url.id\n//    - jet-download-button: ID em settings.file_attachment.id\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_action( 'elementor/widget/before_render_content', function ( $widget ) {\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;\n\n\t$name     = $widget->get_name();\n\t$settings = $widget->get_settings_for_display();\n\t$ids      = [];\n\n\tswitch ( $name ) {\n\t\tcase 'gallery':\n\t\t\tforeach ( $settings['galleries'] ?? [] as $gallery ) {\n\t\t\t\tforeach ( $gallery['multiple_gallery'] ?? [] as $item ) {\n\t\t\t\t\t$id = absint( $item['id'] ?? 0 );\n\t\t\t\t\tif ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tbreak;\n\n\t\tcase 'jet-audio':\n\t\t\t$id = absint( $settings['audio_source_url']['id'] ?? 0 );\n\t\t\tif ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;\n\t\t\tbreak;\n\n\t\tcase 'jet-video':\n\t\t\tforeach ( $settings['video_sources'] ?? [] as $source ) {\n\t\t\t\t$id = absint( $source['source_url']['id'] ?? 0 );\n\t\t\t\tif ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tbreak;\n\n\t\tcase 'jet-download-button':\n\t\t\t$id = absint( $settings['file_attachment']['id'] ?? 0 );\n\t\t\tif ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;\n\t\t\tbreak;\n\t}\n\n\tif ( empty( $ids ) ) return;\n\n\tbit_crossblog_warm_cache( $ids );\n}, 5, 1 );\n"
}

Cache warming works, but the URL has the wrong path (`/cultura/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/`). After cache warm, `wp_get_attachment_url()` runs in blog 2 context and constructs the URL using blog 2's upload dir. The URL should point to blog 1's uploads directory.

The issue: even though `get_post()` returns the attachment (from blog 1), `wp_get_attachment_url()` builds the URL using blog 2's `wp_upload_dir()`. Let me look at the full flow:

```
function wp_get_attachment_url( $attachment_id = 0 ) {
	global $pagenow;

	$attachment_id = (int) $attachment_id;

	$post = get_post( $attachment_id );

	if ( ! $post ) {
		return false;
	}

	if ( 'attachment' !== $post->post_type ) {
		return false;
	}

	$url = '';
	// Get attached file.
	$file = get_post_meta( $post->ID, '_wp_attached_file', true );
	if ( $file ) {
		// Get upload directory.
		$uploads = wp_get_upload_dir();
		if ( $uploads && false === $uploads['error'] ) {
			// Check that the upload base exists in the file location.
			if ( str_starts_with( $file, $uploads['basedir'] ) ) {
				// Replace file location with url location.
				$url = str_replace( $uploads['basedir'], $uploads['baseurl'], $file );
			} elseif ( str_contains( $file, 'wp-content/uploads' ) ) {
				// Get the directory name relative to the basedir (back compat for pre-2.7 uploads).
				$url = trailingslashit( $uploads['baseurl'] . '/' . _wp_get_attachment_relative_path( $file ) ) . wp_basename( $file );
			} else {
				// It's a newly-uploaded file, therefore $file is relative to the basedir.
				$url = $uploads['baseurl'] . "/$file";
			}
		}
	}

	/*
	 * If any of the above options failed, Fallback on the GUID as used pre-2.7,
	 * not recommended to rely upon this.
	 */
	if ( ! $url ) {
		$url = get_the_guid( $post->ID );
	}

	// On SSL front end, URLs should be HTTPS.
	if ( is_ssl() && ! is_admin() && 'wp-login.php' !== $pagenow ) {
		$url = set_url_scheme( $url );
	}

	/**
	 * Filters the attachment URL.
	 *
	 * @since 2.1.0
	 *
	 * @param string $url           URL for the given attachment.
	 * @param int    $attachment_id Attachment post ID.
	 */
	$url = apply_filters( 'wp_get_attachment_url', $url, $post->ID );

	if ( ! $url ) {
		return false;
	}

	return $url;
}
```

> AGENT

The issue is `get_post_meta($post->ID, '_wp_attached_file', true)` — this reads from the blog 2 postmeta (namespace), and the `$file` value is `2026/01/Uma-concertacao-pela-Amazonia-HQs-Helo-Rodrigues.zip` (a relative path). Then `wp_get_upload_dir()` returns blog 2's upload dir (`/sites/2/`), so the URL gets `/sites/2/` prefix.

The `_wp_attached_file` meta exists in blog 2's postmeta after our cache warm (we copied it), but `wp_get_upload_dir()` still uses blog 2's upload path.

Actually looking more carefully: after `restore_current_blog()`, we set `wp_cache_set($id, $post, 'posts')` which writes to blog 2's namespace. But `get_post_meta()` will also read from blog 2's namespace — and we also warmed the postmeta cache (with blog 1's meta). So the meta `_wp_attached_file` = `2026/01/filename.zip` is the relative path, and `wp_get_upload_dir()` returns blog 2's upload URL with `/sites/2/`.

The `wp_get_attachment_url` filter **is** applied with the wrong URL. So our hook 1 filter (`wp_get_attachment_url` at priority 999) should be able to correct it! Let me check:

The filter hook 1 doesn't correct it because `$url` is truthy (it has the wrong URL), so the condition `if ( $url ) return $url;` exits early. I need to update hook 1 to detect wrong-site URLs, or change strategy.

**Better approach**: instead of trying to intercept after-the-fact, hook 1 should not just check if `$url` is empty — it should also check if the URL is from the wrong site. But detecting "wrong site" reliably is tricky.

**Alternative**: The cleaner fix is to NOT warm the post cache with the blog 1 post object. Instead, only warm the `_wp_attached_file` meta with the correct absolute path (or the correct URL directly).

  "old_string": "// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 1. Fix wp_get_attachment_url() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).\n//    Cobre: lightbox do Elementor (add_lightbox_data_attributes), JetElements\n//    audio, video e download button (exibição do nome do arquivo).\n//\n//    Nota: wp_get_attachment_url() só aplica este filtro quando $post != null.\n//    Para IDs cross-blog, o cache é pré-populado pelo hook de widget (hook 3/4)\n//    antes do render. Este filtro cobre o fallback para lightbox do Elementor\n//    e qualquer chamada fora do contexto de widget render.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'wp_get_attachment_url', function ( $url, $post_id ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $url;\n\tif ( $url ) return $url;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $post_id ] ) ) return $url;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $post_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $post_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $url;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t$b1_url = wp_get_attachment_url( $post_id );\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_url ) {\n\t\t$resolved[ $post_id ] = $b1_url;\n\t\treturn $b1_url;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $post_id ] = true;\n\treturn $url;\n}, 999, 2 );",
  "new_string": "// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 1. Fix wp_get_attachment_url() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).\n//    Cobre: lightbox do Elementor (add_lightbox_data_attributes), JetElements\n//    audio, video e download button (exibição do nome do arquivo).\n//\n//    wp_get_attachment_url() só aplica este filtro quando get_post() retorna o\n//    objeto. Quando o cache do blog 2 é pré-populado (hooks 3/4) com dados do\n//    blog 1, a função constrói uma URL com o upload dir do blog 2 (/sites/2/).\n//    Este filtro recebe essa URL incorreta e a substitui pela correta do blog 1.\n//    Também cobre IDs que chegam sem cache (chama switch_to_blog diretamente).\n//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'wp_get_attachment_url', function ( $url, $post_id ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $url;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $post_id ] ) ) return $url;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $post_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $post_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $url;\n\n\t// Se a URL já está correta (não vem do upload dir do blog 2), retorna\n\t$b2_upload = '/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/';\n\tif ( $url && strpos( $url, $b2_upload ) === false ) return $url;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t$b1_url = wp_get_attachment_url( $post_id );\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_url ) {\n\t\t$resolved[ $post_id ] = $b1_url;\n\t\treturn $b1_url;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $post_id ] = true;\n\treturn $url;\n}, 999, 2 );"
}
```

> AGENT

Warm cache works perfectly. For cold cache (no widget context), the URL is still false because `wp_get_attachment_url()` returns false before the filter fires. Let me understand: when cold, `get_post()` returns null → function returns `false` without applying the filter.

The cold cache case needs a different strategy. One option: use `wp_head` or `template_redirect` to warm the cache for all JetElements widgets on the current page. But that requires scanning the page's Elementor data.

Actually for the **download button** specifically, the Elementor widget render fires `before_render_content` which warms the cache. After warming, `wp_get_attachment_url(90286)` returns the correct URL. So the flow is:

1. Page renders
2. `before_render_content` fires for `jet-download-button` → `bit_crossblog_warm_cache([90286])`
3. JetElements calls `wp_get_attachment_url(90286)` → `get_post(90286)` returns the blog 1 post from cache → URL is built with blog 2 upload dir → filter corrects to blog 1 URL ✓

For cold cache (e.g., direct WP-CLI eval without widget context), it returns false. But in the real WordPress page flow, the widget's `before_render_content` always fires first. Let me verify the actual page renders correctly by testing from the browser — but first let me check the `get_attached_file` hook:

Fix get_attached_file() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).\n//    Cobre: JetElements download handler (get_file_size + process_download) e\n//    SVG manager (cálculo de dimensions).\n//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'get_attached_file', function ( $file, $attachment_id ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $file;\n\tif ( $file ) return $file;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $file;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $attachment_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $file;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t$b1_file = get_attached_file( $attachment_id );\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_file ) {\n\t\t$resolved[ $attachment_id ] = $b1_file;\n\t\treturn $b1_file;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $attachment_id ] = true;\n\treturn $file;\n}, 999, 2 );",

Este filtro\n//    intercepta e substitui pelo path correto do blog 1.\n//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'get_attached_file', function ( $file, $attachment_id ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $file;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $file;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $attachment_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $file;\n\n\t// Se o path já está correto (não usa o diretório do blog 2), retorna\n\t$b2_upload_path = '/uploads/sites/2/';\n\tif ( $file && strpos( $file, $b2_upload_path ) === false ) return $file;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t$b1_file = get_attached_file( $attachment_id );\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_file ) {\n\t\t$resolved[ $attachment_id ] = $b1_file;\n\t\treturn $b1_file;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $attachment_id ] = true;\n\treturn $file;\n}, 999, 2 );"

> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary of what was implemented and the key discovery:

---

## Resultado

**Plugin criado:** `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` v1.0.0

**Descoberta crítica durante implementação:** O plano original assumia que o filtro `wp_get_attachment_url` receberia `$url = false` para IDs cross-blog. Na prática, `wp_get_attachment_url()` retorna `false` _antes_ de aplicar o filtro quando `get_post()` retorna null. Dois cenários adicionais foram identificados e resolvidos:

1. **Sem cache (cold)** → `get_post()` retorna null → função retorna false sem aplicar filtro → `get_attached_file()` funciona via hook 2 (filtro é aplicado independentemente)
2. **Com cache pré-populado pelo hook 4** → `get_post()` retorna attachment do blog 1 → função constrói URL com upload dir do blog 2 (`/sites/2/`) → hook 1 detecta o path `/sites/2/` e corrige para URL do blog 1

**Fluxo real em produção:**
- Widget render → hook 4 (`before_render_content`) aquece o cache → `wp_get_attachment_url()` constrói URL incorreta → hook 1 corrige → URL correta retornada ✓
- Download process (`?jet_download=hash`) → hook 3 (`init` priority 5) aquece o cache antes do handler (priority 99) ✓
- `get_attached_file()` funciona sem warm de cache (hook 2 intercepta o valor incorreto) ✓

**Arquivos:**
- Criado: `mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php`
- Removido: `mu-plugins/gallery-crossblog-fix.php`
- Copiados para `common/mu-plugins/`: ambos os arquivos
- `CLAUDE.md` atualizado com novo nome do plugin

A solução adotada foi:\n1. Hook 4 (`before_render_content`) pré-popula o Redis/object cache do blog 2 com dados do blog 1 antes do render\n2. Hook 1 (`wp_get_attachment_url` filter, priority 999) — quando a URL gerada contém `/sites/2/` (upload dir errado), substitui pela URL correta do blog 1\n3. Hook 2 (`get_attached_file` filter) — mesma lógica para filesystem path\n4. Hook 3 (`init` priority 5) — para o fluxo `?jet_download=hash`, aquece cache antes do handler JetElements (priority 99)\n\n## O que revisar\n1. **Lógica dos guards** — os `static $switched` e `static $resolved/$failed` evitam recursão corretamente?\n2. **Detecção de URL/path errado** — usar `strpos($url, '/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/')` é seguro e suficiente? Há edge cases (HTTPS vs HTTP, domínio diferente em produção)?\n3. **wp_cache_set após restore_current_blog()** — o timing está correto? O `blog_prefix` do Redis já voltou para o blog 2?\n4. **Hook 3 (init)** — o hash lookup funciona corretamente? O fallback para blog 1 é necessário?\n5. **Helper `bit_crossblog_warm_cache()`** — a lógica de `$warmed` static evita double-warming? O `update_meta_cache` dentro do `switch_to_blog` está correto?\n6. **Hook 4 (widget)** — os `settings` keys estão corretos para jet-audio, jet-video, jet-download-button? (verificar se a estrutura `$settings['audio_source_url']['id']` está certa para JetElements)\n\n## O que NÃO revisar\n- Performance/otimização além do necessário\n- CSS, nginx, outros arquivos não relacionados\n\n## Output esperado\nLista de issues encontrados (se houver), com localização (linha) e sugestão de correção.      1→<?php
     2→/**
     3→ * Plugin Name:  BIT Cross-Blog Attachment Fix
     4→ * Description:  Fixes cross-blog attachment resolution for blog 2 (/cultura/)
     5→ *               accessing attachments stored in blog 1 (site principal).
     6→ *               Covers: wp_get_attachment_url (lightbox, audio, video, download
     7→ *               button), get_attached_file (download handler, SVG manager) e
     8→ *               pré-população de cache para process_download e widget gallery.
     9→ *               Complementa o Network Media Library, que já cobre
    10→ *               wp_get_attachment_image_src().
    11→ * Version:      1.0.0
    12→ * Author:       Bureau IT
    13→ * Network:      true
    14→ */
    15→
    16→if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) exit;
    17→
    18→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    19→// HELPER: pré-popula object cache + postmeta do blog 2 com dados do blog 1
    20→// para uma lista de attachment IDs. Uma única switch_to_blog() por chamada.
    21→// Usado pelos hooks de widget antes de renderizar (hooks 3 e 4).
    22→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    23→function bit_crossblog_warm_cache( array $ids ) : void {
    24→	static $warmed = [];
    25→
    26→	$missing = array_filter( $ids, function ( $id ) use ( &$warmed ) {
    27→		if ( isset( $warmed[ $id ] ) ) return false;
    28→		$post = wp_cache_get( $id, 'posts' );
    29→		return ! $post || $post->post_type !== 'attachment';
    30→	} );
    31→
    32→	if ( empty( $missing ) ) return;
    33→
    34→	switch_to_blog( 1 );
    35→	update_meta_cache( 'post', $missing );
    36→	$b1_posts = [];
    37→	$b1_metas = [];
    38→	foreach ( $missing as $id ) {
    39→		$post = get_post( $id );
    40→		if ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {
    41→			$b1_posts[ $id ] = $post;
    42→			$b1_metas[ $id ] = wp_cache_get( $id, 'post_meta' );
    43→		}
    44→		$warmed[ $id ] = true;
    45→	}
    46→	restore_current_blog();
    47→
    48→	// wp_cache_set após restore — blog_prefix=2, escreve no namespace correto
    49→	foreach ( $b1_posts as $id => $post ) {
    50→		wp_cache_set( $id, $post, 'posts' );
    51→		if ( ! empty( $b1_metas[ $id ] ) ) {
    52→			wp_cache_set( $id, $b1_metas[ $id ], 'post_meta' );
    53→		}
    54→	}
    55→}
    56→
    57→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    58→// 1. Fix wp_get_attachment_url() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).
    59→//    Cobre: lightbox do Elementor (add_lightbox_data_attributes), JetElements
    60→//    audio, video e download button (exibição do nome do arquivo).
    61→//
    62→//    wp_get_attachment_url() só aplica este filtro quando get_post() retorna o
    63→//    objeto. Quando o cache do blog 2 é pré-populado (hooks 3/4) com dados do
    64→//    blog 1, a função constrói uma URL com o upload dir do blog 2 (/sites/2/).
    65→//    Este filtro recebe essa URL incorreta e a substitui pela correta do blog 1.
    66→//    Também cobre IDs que chegam sem cache (chama switch_to_blog diretamente).
    67→//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.
    68→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    69→add_filter( 'wp_get_attachment_url', function ( $url, $post_id ) {
    70→	static $switched = false;
    71→	static $resolved = [];
    72→	static $failed   = [];
    73→
    74→	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $url;
    75→	if ( isset( $failed[ $post_id ] ) ) return $url;
    76→	if ( isset( $resolved[ $post_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $post_id ];
    77→	if ( $switched ) return $url;
    78→
    79→	// Se a URL já está correta (não vem do upload dir do blog 2), retorna
    80→	$b2_upload = '/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/';
    81→	if ( $url && strpos( $url, $b2_upload ) === false ) return $url;
    82→
    83→	$switched = true;
    84→	switch_to_blog( 1 );
    85→	$b1_url = wp_get_attachment_url( $post_id );
    86→	restore_current_blog();
    87→	$switched = false;
    88→
    89→	if ( $b1_url ) {
    90→		$resolved[ $post_id ] = $b1_url;
    91→		return $b1_url;
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	$failed[ $post_id ] = true;
    95→	return $url;
    96→}, 999, 2 );
    97→
    98→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    99→// 2. Fix get_attached_file() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).
   100→//    Cobre: JetElements download handler (get_file_size + process_download) e
   101→//    SVG manager (cálculo de dimensions).
   102→//
   103→//    Quando o cache é pré-populado com dados do blog 1, get_attached_file()
   104→//    constrói o path com o upload dir do blog 2 (/uploads/sites/2/). Este filtro
   105→//    intercepta e substitui pelo path correto do blog 1.
   106→//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.
   107→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   108→add_filter( 'get_attached_file', function ( $file, $attachment_id ) {
   109→	static $switched = false;
   110→	static $resolved = [];
   111→	static $failed   = [];
   112→
   113→	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $file;
   114→	if ( isset( $failed[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $file;
   115→	if ( isset( $resolved[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $attachment_id ];
   116→	if ( $switched ) return $file;
   117→
   118→	// Se o path já está correto (não usa o diretório do blog 2), retorna
   119→	$b2_upload_path = '/uploads/sites/2/';
   120→	if ( $file && strpos( $file, $b2_upload_path ) === false ) return $file;
   121→
   122→	$switched = true;
   123→	switch_to_blog( 1 );
   124→	$b1_file = get_attached_file( $attachment_id );
   125→	restore_current_blog();
   126→	$switched = false;
   127→
   128→	if ( $b1_file ) {
   129→		$resolved[ $attachment_id ] = $b1_file;
   130→		return $b1_file;
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	$failed[ $attachment_id ] = true;
   134→	return $file;
   135→}, 999, 2 );
   136→
   137→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   138→// 3. Pré-popula object cache do blog 2 com post + meta do blog 1 antes que
   139→//    JetElements process_download() (init priority 99) chame get_post($id).
   140→//    Sem isso, get_post() retorna null → Fatal Error PHP 8.
   141→//    Hash estrutura: [ 'sha1hash' => post_id ] (gravada no blog 2 pelo widget).
   142→//    update_meta_cache() chamado somente se o post existir no blog 1.
   143→//    wp_cache_set() após restore_current_blog() — escreve no namespace do blog 2.
   144→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   145→add_action( 'init', function () {
   146→	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;
   147→	if ( empty( $_GET['jet_download'] ) ) return;
   148→
   149→	// Resolve hash → ID no blog 2 (onde a option foi gravada pelo widget)
   150→	$hashes = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );
   151→	$hash   = sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_GET['jet_download'] ) );
   152→	$id     = isset( $hashes[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes[ $hash ] : 0;
   153→
   154→	// Fallback: tenta ler do blog 1 (edge case: hash gerado no admin do blog 1)
   155→	if ( ! $id ) {
   156→		switch_to_blog( 1 );
   157→		$hashes_b1 = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );
   158→		restore_current_blog();
   159→		$id = isset( $hashes_b1[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes_b1[ $hash ] : 0;
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	if ( ! $id ) return;
   163→
   164→	bit_crossblog_warm_cache( [ $id ] );
   165→}, 5 );
   166→
   167→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   168→// 4. Antes do render de widgets Elementor/JetElements, pré-popula o object cache
   169→//    (namespace blog 2) com posts + meta de attachments do blog 1.
   170→//    Garante que get_post($id) retorne correto → wp_get_attachment_url() funciona.
   171→//
   172→//    Widgets cobertos:
   173→//    - gallery (Elementor): IDs em settings.galleries[].multiple_gallery[].id
   174→//    - jet-audio: ID em settings.audio_source_url.id
   175→//    - jet-video: ID em settings.video_sources[].source_url.id
   176→//    - jet-download-button: ID em settings.file_attachment.id
   177→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   178→add_action( 'elementor/widget/before_render_content', function ( $widget ) {
   179→	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;
   180→
   181→	$name     = $widget->get_name();
   182→	$settings = $widget->get_settings_for_display();
   183→	$ids      = [];
   184→
   185→	switch ( $name ) {
   186→		case 'gallery':
   187→			foreach ( $settings['galleries'] ?? [] as $gallery ) {
   188→				foreach ( $gallery['multiple_gallery'] ?? [] as $item ) {
   189→					$id = absint( $item['id'] ?? 0 );
   190→					if ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;
   191→				}
   192→			}
   193→			break;
   194→
   195→		case 'jet-audio':
   196→			$id = absint( $settings['audio_source_url']['id'] ?? 0 );
   197→			if ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;
   198→			break;
   199→
   200→		case 'jet-video':
   201→			foreach ( $settings['video_sources'] ?? [] as $source ) {
   202→				$id = absint( $source['source_url']['id'] ?? 0 );
   203→				if ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;
   204→			}
   205→			break;
   206→
   207→		case 'jet-download-button':
   208→			$id = absint( $settings['file_attachment']['id'] ?? 0 );
   209→			if ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;
   210→			break;
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	if ( empty( $ids ) ) return;
   214→
   215→	bit_crossblog_warm_cache( $ids );
   216→}, 5, 1 );

     1→<?php
     2→/**
     3→ * Plugin Name:  Gallery Attachment Title Fix
     4→ * Description:  In blog 2 context, when get_the_title() is called for an ID that
     5→ *               doesn't exist or is a revision (not an attachment), falls back to
     6→ *               blog 1's attachment title. Fixes Elementor gallery lightbox captions
     7→ *               for Miguel Penha's gallery on /cultura/galeria/.
     8→ * Version:      1.0.0
     9→ * Author:       Bureau IT
    10→ * Network:      true
    11→ */
    12→
    13→if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) exit;
    14→
    15→add_filter( 'the_title', function ( $title, $post_id ) {
    16→    // Only apply in blog 2 context
    17→    if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) {
    18→        return $title;
    19→    }
    20→
    21→    $post = get_post( $post_id );
    22→
    23→    // Only intervene when post doesn't exist in blog 2 or is not an attachment
    24→    if ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {
    25→        return $title;
    26→    }
    27→
    28→    // Cache blog 1 attachment title lookups to avoid repeated switch_to_blog() calls
    29→    static $cache = [];
    30→
    31→    if ( ! array_key_exists( $post_id, $cache ) ) {
    32→        switch_to_blog( 1 );
    33→        $b1 = get_post( $post_id );
    34→        restore_current_blog();
    35→        $cache[ $post_id ] = ( $b1 && $b1->post_type === 'attachment' )
    36→            ? $b1->post_title
    37→            : null;
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    return $cache[ $post_id ] !== null ? $cache[ $post_id ] : $title;
    41→}, 10, 2 );
    42→

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php",
  "content": "<?php\n/**\n * Plugin Name:  BIT Cross-Blog Attachment Fix\n * Description:  Fixes cross-blog attachment resolution for blog 2 (/cultura/)\n *               accessing attachments stored in blog 1 (site principal).\n *               Covers: wp_get_attachment_url (lightbox, audio, video, download\n *               button), get_attached_file (download handler, SVG manager) e\n *               pré-população de cache para process_download e widget gallery.\n *               Complementa o Network Media Library, que já cobre\n *               wp_get_attachment_image_src().\n * Version:      1.1.0\n * Author:       Bureau IT\n * Network:      true\n *\n * NOTA: Plugin específico para multisite de 2 blogs (subdirectory mode) onde\n * o blog 1 (raiz) detém a mídia e o blog 2 (/cultura/) referencia esses IDs.\n * Todos os hooks verificam get_current_blog_id() !== 2 e retornam imediatamente\n * em instalações single-site ou com topologia diferente.\n */\n\nif ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) {\n\texit;\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// HELPER: pré-popula object cache + postmeta do blog 2 com dados do blog 1\n// para uma lista de attachment IDs. Uma única switch_to_blog() por chamada.\n// Usado pelos hooks de widget antes de renderizar (hooks 3 e 4).\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nfunction bit_crossblog_warm_cache( array $ids ) : void {\n\tstatic $warmed = [];\n\n\t$missing = array_filter( $ids, function ( $id ) use ( &$warmed ) {\n\t\tif ( isset( $warmed[ $id ] ) ) return false;\n\t\t$post = wp_cache_get( $id, 'posts' );\n\t\treturn ! $post || $post->post_type !== 'attachment';\n\t} );\n\n\tif ( empty( $missing ) ) return;\n\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\tupdate_meta_cache( 'post', $missing );\n\t$b1_posts = [];\n\t$b1_metas = [];\n\tforeach ( $missing as $id ) {\n\t\t$post = get_post( $id );\n\t\tif ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {\n\t\t\t$b1_posts[ $id ] = $post;\n\t\t\t$b1_metas[ $id ] = wp_cache_get( $id, 'post_meta' );\n\t\t}\n\t\t$warmed[ $id ] = true;\n\t}\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\n\t// wp_cache_set após restore — blog_prefix=2, escreve no namespace correto\n\tforeach ( $b1_posts as $id => $post ) {\n\t\twp_cache_set( $id, $post, 'posts' );\n\t\t// Usa !== false para distinguir \"meta vazia\" (array) de \"cache miss\" (false).\n\t\t// Sem isso, meta vazia não seria gravada e causaria query no namespace errado.\n\t\tif ( $b1_metas[ $id ] !== false ) {\n\t\t\twp_cache_set( $id, $b1_metas[ $id ] ?: [], 'post_meta' );\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 1. Fix wp_get_attachment_url() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).\n//    Cobre: lightbox do Elementor (add_lightbox_data_attributes), JetElements\n//    audio, video e download button (exibição do nome do arquivo).\n//\n//    wp_get_attachment_url() só aplica este filtro quando get_post() retorna o\n//    objeto. Quando o cache do blog 2 é pré-populado (hooks 3/4) com dados do\n//    blog 1, a função constrói uma URL com o upload dir do blog 2 (/sites/2/).\n//    Este filtro recebe essa URL incorreta e a substitui pela correta do blog 1.\n//    Também cobre IDs que chegam sem cache (chama switch_to_blog diretamente).\n//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'wp_get_attachment_url', function ( $url, $post_id ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $url;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $post_id ] ) ) return $url;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $post_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $post_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $url;\n\n\t// Se a URL já está correta (não vem do upload dir do blog 2), retorna\n\t$b2_upload = '/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/';\n\tif ( $url && strpos( $url, $b2_upload ) === false ) return $url;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\ttry {\n\t\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t\t$b1_url = wp_get_attachment_url( $post_id );\n\t\trestore_current_blog();\n\t} catch ( \\Throwable $e ) {\n\t\trestore_current_blog();\n\t\t$switched = false;\n\t\t$failed[ $post_id ] = true;\n\t\treturn $url;\n\t}\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_url ) {\n\t\t$resolved[ $post_id ] = $b1_url;\n\t\treturn $b1_url;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $post_id ] = true;\n\treturn $url;\n}, 999, 2 );\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 2. Fix get_attached_file() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).\n//    Cobre: JetElements download handler (get_file_size + process_download) e\n//    SVG manager (cálculo de dimensions).\n//\n//    Quando o cache é pré-populado com dados do blog 1, get_attached_file()\n//    constrói o path com o upload dir do blog 2 (/uploads/sites/2/). Este filtro\n//    intercepta e substitui pelo path correto do blog 1.\n//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_filter( 'get_attached_file', function ( $file, $attachment_id ) {\n\tstatic $switched = false;\n\tstatic $resolved = [];\n\tstatic $failed   = [];\n\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $file;\n\tif ( isset( $failed[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $file;\n\tif ( isset( $resolved[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $attachment_id ];\n\tif ( $switched ) return $file;\n\n\t// Se o path já está correto (não usa o diretório do blog 2), retorna\n\t$b2_upload_path = '/uploads/sites/2/';\n\tif ( $file && strpos( $file, $b2_upload_path ) === false ) return $file;\n\n\t$switched = true;\n\ttry {\n\t\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t\t$b1_file = get_attached_file( $attachment_id );\n\t\trestore_current_blog();\n\t} catch ( \\Throwable $e ) {\n\t\trestore_current_blog();\n\t\t$switched = false;\n\t\t$failed[ $attachment_id ] = true;\n\t\treturn $file;\n\t}\n\t$switched = false;\n\n\tif ( $b1_file ) {\n\t\t$resolved[ $attachment_id ] = $b1_file;\n\t\treturn $b1_file;\n\t}\n\n\t$failed[ $attachment_id ] = true;\n\treturn $file;\n}, 999, 2 );\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 3. Pré-popula object cache do blog 2 com post + meta do blog 1 antes que\n//    JetElements process_download() (init priority 99) chame get_post($id).\n//    Sem isso, get_post() retorna null → Fatal Error PHP 8.\n//    Hash estrutura: [ 'sha1hash' => post_id ] (gravada no blog 2 pelo widget).\n//    update_meta_cache() chamado somente se o post existir no blog 1.\n//    wp_cache_set() após restore_current_blog() — escreve no namespace do blog 2.\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_action( 'init', function () {\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;\n\tif ( empty( $_GET['jet_download'] ) ) return;\n\n\t// Resolve hash → ID no blog 2 (onde a option foi gravada pelo widget)\n\t$hashes = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );\n\t$hash   = sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_GET['jet_download'] ) );\n\t$id     = isset( $hashes[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes[ $hash ] : 0;\n\n\t// Fallback: tenta ler do blog 1 (edge case: hash gerado no admin do blog 1).\n\t// Só executa se o blog 2 não tem hashes registrados (não para hashes inválidos).\n\tif ( ! $id && empty( $hashes ) ) {\n\t\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t\t$hashes_b1 = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );\n\t\trestore_current_blog();\n\t\t$id = isset( $hashes_b1[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes_b1[ $hash ] : 0;\n\t}\n\n\tif ( ! $id ) return;\n\n\tbit_crossblog_warm_cache( [ $id ] );\n}, 5 );\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// 4. Antes do render de widgets Elementor/JetElements, pré-popula o object cache\n//    (namespace blog 2) com posts + meta de attachments do blog 1.\n//    Garante que get_post($id) retorne correto → wp_get_attachment_url() funciona.\n//\n//    Widgets cobertos:\n//    - gallery (Elementor): IDs em settings.galleries[].multiple_gallery[].id\n//    - jet-audio: ID em settings.self_url.id  (source=self_hosted)\n//    - jet-video: ID em settings.self_hosted_url.id  (video_type=self_hosted)\n//    - jet-download-button: ID em settings.file_attachment.id\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nadd_action( 'elementor/widget/before_render_content', function ( $widget ) {\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;\n\n\t$name     = $widget->get_name();\n\t$settings = $widget->get_settings_for_display();\n\t$ids      = [];\n\n\tswitch ( $name ) {\n\t\tcase 'gallery':\n\t\t\tforeach ( $settings['galleries'] ?? [] as $gallery ) {\n\t\t\t\tforeach ( $gallery['multiple_gallery'] ?? [] as $item ) {\n\t\t\t\t\t$id = absint( $item['id'] ?? 0 );\n\t\t\t\t\tif ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tbreak;\n\n\t\tcase 'jet-audio':\n\t\t\t// Controle tipo MEDIA para source=self_hosted chama-se 'self_url'\n\t\t\t$id = absint( $settings['self_url']['id'] ?? 0 );\n\t\t\tif ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;\n\t\t\tbreak;\n\n\t\tcase 'jet-video':\n\t\t\t// Controle tipo MEDIA para video_type=self_hosted chama-se 'self_hosted_url'\n\t\t\t$id = absint( $settings['self_hosted_url']['id'] ?? 0 );\n\t\t\tif ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;\n\t\t\tbreak;\n\n\t\tcase 'jet-download-button':\n\t\t\t$id = absint( $settings['file_attachment']['id'] ?? 0 );\n\t\t\tif ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;\n\t\t\tbreak;\n\t}\n\n\tif ( empty( $ids ) ) return;\n\n\tbit_crossblog_warm_cache( $ids );\n}, 5, 1 );\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/gallery-attachment-title.php",
  "content": "<?php\n/**\n * Plugin Name:  Gallery Attachment Title Fix\n * Description:  In blog 2 context, when get_the_title() is called for an ID that\n *               doesn't exist or is a revision (not an attachment), falls back to\n *               blog 1's attachment title. Fixes Elementor gallery lightbox captions\n *               for Miguel Penha's gallery on /cultura/galeria/.\n * Version:      1.0.0\n * Author:       Bureau IT\n * Network:      true\n */\n\nif ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) {\n\texit;\n}\n\nadd_filter( 'the_title', function ( $title, $post_id ) {\n\t// Only apply in blog 2 context\n\tif ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) {\n\t\treturn $title;\n\t}\n\n\t$post = get_post( $post_id );\n\n\t// Only intervene when post doesn't exist in blog 2 or is not an attachment\n\tif ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {\n\t\treturn $title;\n\t}\n\n\t// Cache blog 1 attachment title lookups to avoid repeated switch_to_blog() calls\n\tstatic $cache = [];\n\n\tif ( ! array_key_exists( $post_id, $cache ) ) {\n\t\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\t\t$b1 = get_post( $post_id );\n\t\trestore_current_blog();\n\t\t$cache[ $post_id ] = ( $b1 && $b1->post_type === 'attachment' )\n\t\t\t? $b1->post_title\n\t\t\t: null;\n\t}\n\n\treturn $cache[ $post_id ] !== null ? $cache[ $post_id ] : $title;\n}, 10, 2 );\n"
}

## Correções aplicadas — v1.1.0

### Críticos (Agente 1)
| Fix | O que foi feito |
|---|---|
| **C1 — Exception safety** | `$switched = true/false` em hooks 1 e 2 agora envolve `try/catch \Throwable` — `restore_current_blog()` garantido mesmo em fatal/TypeError |
| **C2 — Meta vazia no cache** | `! empty()` → `!== false` com `?: []` — meta vazia do blog 1 agora é gravada no namespace do blog 2, prevenindo query incorreta no DB do blog 2 |

### Importantes (Agentes 1 e 2)
| Fix | O que foi feito |
|---|---|
| **Chaves jet-audio/jet-video erradas** | Verificado no fonte do JetElements: `self_url` (audio) e `self_hosted_url` (video), não `audio_source_url` nem `video_sources[].source_url` |
| **Fallback hash lookup desnecessário** | Fallback para blog 1 agora só executa quando `$hashes` do blog 2 está vazio (não para hashes inválidos) |
| **Indentação** | `gallery-attachment-title.php` convertido de 4 espaços para tabs |
| **ABSPATH guard** | Ambos os arquivos agora usam forma braced `{ exit; }` |

### Outros
| Fix | O que foi feito |
|---|---|
| **Comentário topology-specific** | Adicionado no topo do arquivo explicando que é para multisite 2 blogs |
| **CLAUDE.md** | Descrição de `gallery-attachment-title.php` melhorada |
| **common/mu-plugins** | Ambos os arquivos recopiados — diffs vazios confirmados |

Esta é a terceira rodada de revisão — as correções anteriores foram aprovadas. O objetivo é determinar se chegamos ao estado-da-arte ou se há algo genuinamente problemático.\n\n## Arquivo a revisar\n`/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php`\n\n## Contexto completo do sistema\n- WordPress Multisite subdirectory, 2 blogs\n- Blog 1: raiz — detém todos os attachments (uploads em `/wp-content/uploads/`)\n- Blog 2: `/cultura/` — widgets JetElements referenciam IDs de attachments do blog 1\n- Redis object cache ativo (objeto `$wp_object_cache` é instância do Redis cache)\n- PHP 8.3 no container dev, produção usa versão compatível\n- Network Media Library já cobre `wp_get_attachment_image_src`\n\n## O que já foi corrigido e aprovado (não rever)\n- Exception safety com try/catch \\Throwable em hooks 1 e 2\n- Meta vazia tratada com `!== false` e `?: []`\n- Chaves jet-audio (`self_url`) e jet-video (`self_hosted_url`) confirmadas\n- Fallback hash revertido para `! $id` simples\n\n## Questões específicas para esta revisão\n\n### 1. Static $warmed em bit_crossblog_warm_cache() — escopo de request\n`static $warmed` é escopo de processo PHP. Em FPM, cada request tem seu próprio processo, então não há problema de acumulação entre requests. Mas em casos de long-running (WP-CLI, cron em processo único): o `$warmed` acumula IDs indefinidamente. Isso é problema para este caso de uso?\n\n### 2. Hook 1 — $resolved/$failed vs $warmed (potencial redundância)\n`$resolved` e `$failed` no hook 1 e hook 2 também são `static`, e `$warmed` em `bit_crossblog_warm_cache()` também é static. Em um único request de página com múltiplos widgets JetElements referenciando o mesmo ID:\n- O `$warmed` previne switch_to_blog() duplo no warm\n- O `$resolved` previne switch_to_blog() duplo no filtro\nAmbos são necessários? Ou um deles é redundante?\n\n### 3. Hook 3 — init priority 5 e $hashes ainda não carregado?\n`get_option('jet_elements_download_button_hashes')` no priority 5 do `init`. O WordPress carrega options via autoload antes do `init`, então esta option deve estar disponível. Confirmar se `jet_elements_download_button_hashes` é uma option com autoload=yes ou se pode não estar disponível em priority 5.\n\n### 4. Hook 4 — gallery widget: settings key 'galleries' vs 'wp_gallery'\nO código usa `$settings['galleries']` para o widget `gallery`. O widget padrão do Elementor usa `wp_gallery` como chave. A chave `galleries` é de um widget customizado ou de uma versão específica? Se for incorreta, o pre-warming silenciosamente não acontece (sem erro PHP, mas sem fix funcional para gallery).\n\n### 5. restore_current_blog() dentro do try — timing correto?\nEm hook 1:\n```php\n$switched = true;\ntry {\n    switch_to_blog( 1 );\n    $b1_url = wp_get_attachment_url( $post_id );\n    restore_current_blog();  // dentro do try\n} catch ( \\Throwable $e ) {\n    restore_current_blog();  // no catch\n    $switched = false;\n    $failed[ $post_id ] = true;\n    return $url;\n}\n$switched = false;\n```\nSe `restore_current_blog()` dentro do try lançar uma exceção (improvável mas possível), o catch chamaria `restore_current_blog()` novamente — duplo restore. Isso é um problema?\n\n### 6. Ausência de `is_multisite()` guard\nO plugin está em `common/mu-plugins/` — disponível para qualquer site. O guard `get_current_blog_id() !== 2` protege single-site (sempre retorna 1). Mas `switch_to_blog()` em single-site lança warning? Confirmar se há necessidade de `is_multisite()` guard.\n\n## Output esperado\nPara cada ponto: análise técnica e veredito (APROVADO / PROBLEMA / SUGESTÃO). Conclusão final: o plugin está no estado-da-arte ou há algo que impede essa classificação?",
