Hook 3 — sanitização do $_GET['jet_download']\n```php\n$hash = sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_GET['jet_download'] ) );\n```\n`sanitize_text_field` remove tags HTML e espaços extras. Há algum risco de prototype pollution ou array injection aqui? O cast `(int)` protege o valor de retorno.\n\n### 3. switch_to_blog(1) — hardcoded\nO blog ID 1 está hardcoded. Em um ataque de SSRF ou traversal, isso poderia ser explorado? Contexto: o plugin roda server-side dentro de WordPress, não expõe o blog ID ao usuário.\n\n### 4. wp_cache_set após restore_current_blog — namespace isolation\nApós `restore_current_blog()`, o Redis namespace volta para blog 2. `wp_cache_set($id, $post, 'posts')` grava com o blog_prefix do blog 2. Confirmar: isso significa que o dado do blog 1 é gravado no namespace do blog 2 intencionalmente? Há risco de \"cache poisoning\" — onde um dado do blog 1 contamina permanentemente o namespace do blog 2 com dados incorretos para um ID que genuinamente existe no blog 2?\n\n## Performance\n\n### 5. switch_to_blog() — custo real\n`switch_to_blog()` faz: salva blog atual, altera `$wpdb->prefix`, altera `$current_blog`, altera `$current_site`, muda o blog_prefix do object cache. Para este plugin, em um request de página típico com 1 download button widget, quantas vezes `switch_to_blog()` é chamado no máximo? (contar: hook 3 + hook 4 + hooks 1/2 via filtros)\n\n### 6. update_meta_cache() — custo quando IDs já estão no cache\nEm `bit_crossblog_warm_cache()`, `update_meta_cache('post', $missing)` é chamado no contexto do blog 1. Se os IDs já estiverem no cache do blog 1 (Redis hit), `update_meta_cache` ainda faz query SQL? Ou é no-op?\n\n### 7. Hook 1 e 2 priority 999 — conflito com outros plugins?\nPriority 999 é muito alta. Se outro plugin (NML, Elementor Pro, JetElements) também registrar filtros em `wp_get_attachment_url` ou `get_attached_file` com priority > 999, eles seriam executados após este plugin. Isso é um problema? Verificar se NML ou Elementor registram filtros nessas funções com prioridade alta.\n\n## Output esperado\nPara cada ponto: análise e veredito. Conclusão: o plugin está no estado-da-arte de segurança e performance, ou há gaps?",
  "run_in_background": true
}

## Resultado das revisões — veredito final

### Agente 1 — Lógica e edge cases: todos aprovados

| Ponto | Veredito |
|---|---|
| `static $warmed` em long-running | APROVADO — crescimento finito, comportamento intencional |
| `$resolved`/`$failed` vs `$warmed` — redundância? | APROVADO — três camadas com papéis distintos, todas necessárias |
| `init` priority 5 e autoload | APROVADO — options carregadas antes de qualquer hook |
| Chave `galleries` vs `wp_gallery` | SUGESTÃO aplicada → comentário adicionado no `case 'gallery'` |
| `finally` vs duplo `restore_current_blog` | SUGESTÃO aplicada → migrado para `try/catch/finally` com `$b1_url = false` |
| `is_multisite()` guard | APROVADO — guard `!== 2` é suficiente, header documenta escopo |

### Agente 2 — Segurança e performance: todos aprovados

| Ponto | Veredito |
|---|---|
| Sanitização `$_GET['jet_download']` | APROVADO — hash é só array key, sem SQL/eval/path |
| Hash como array key | APROVADO — PHP não tem prototype pollution, `(int)` protege valor |
| `switch_to_blog(1)` hardcoded | APROVADO — server-side, sem exposição, correto para o escopo |
| Cache poisoning via `wp_cache_set` | APROVADO — `$missing` filtra IDs já em cache com tipo correto |
| Custo de `switch_to_blog()` por request | APROVADO — máximo 1 no caso típico, static caches evitam repetição |
| `update_meta_cache` com Redis hit | APROVADO — no-op comprovado no core (retorna sem query SQL) |
| Priority 999 — conflitos | APROVADO — único outro filtro é `tunnel-url-rewrite.php` em priority 10 (sem conflito) |

### Agente 3 — Completude: aprovado com documentação aplicada

| Ponto | Veredito |
|---|---|
| Cobertura de widgets JetElements | APROVADO — todos os addons que chamam as funções estão cobertos |
| `gallery_type=single` | GAP TEÓRICO — modo não utilizado no site; comentário documentado |
| `gallery-attachment-title.php` — alt/caption | APROVADO — warm_cache já popula via object cache |
| `wp_get_attachment_metadata()` | FORA DE ESCOPO — não chamada nos widgets cobertos |
| Documentação `gallery-attachment-title.php` | APLICADO → header esclarece qual caminho cobre que o warm_cache não cobre |

Os únicos gaps identificados são teóricos (modo `gallery_type=single` não utilizado, `wp_get_attachment_metadata` fora de escopo) e estão documentados no código.

Análise concluída. Aqui está a avaliação:

---

## Recomendação: MANTER SEPARADOS

### Justificativa técnica

**1. Coesão real existe, mas os problemas resolvidos operam em camadas distintas.**
O `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` trabalha na camada de *resolução de recursos* (URLs, caminhos de arquivo, cache de objeto) via hooks de baixo nível (`wp_get_attachment_url`, `get_attached_file`, `init`, `elementor/widget/before_render_content`). O `gallery-attachment-title.php` trabalha na camada de *apresentação* (`the_title`). Embora compartilhem o mesmo problema raiz (blog 2 referenciando attachments do blog 1), são interceptações em pontos completamente diferentes do ciclo de vida de uma request. Consolidar mistura responsabilidades que hoje estão bem delimitadas.

**2. O filtro `the_title` tem lógica de fallback independente e mais simples — mesclar criaria acoplamento desnecessário.**
O plugin 2 faz seu próprio `switch_to_blog(1)` com `static $cache` próprio, sem depender de `bit_crossblog_warm_cache()`. Isso é intencional: ele cobre o caminho onde o cache *não foi* pré-populado. Se fosse movido para dentro do plugin 1, a tentação natural na manutenção seria reescrever o fallback para reusar `warm_cache`, quebrando exatamente o caso de uso para o qual ele foi criado (chamada direta a `get_the_title()` fora do contexto de render de widget).

**3. O argumento de `common/mu-plugins/` pesa contra a fusão.**
O plugin 1 é específico para multisite com topologia blog 1 / blog 2. O plugin 2 é ainda mais específico: resolve um caso pontual de legendas de lightbox em `/cultura/galeria/`. Em outros sites do `common/mu-plugins/`, ativar o plugin 2 junto com o 1 seria forçar dois comportamentos que nem sempre andam juntos. Arquivos separados permitem composição granular por site.

**4. Tamanho e risco de regressão na fusão são baixos em absoluto, mas o ganho também é mínimo.**
O plugin 2 tem apenas 48 linhas. O plugin consolidado teria ~290 linhas — gerenciável, mas sem nenhum benefício técnico concreto: não há função compartilhada a extrair (os `static $cache` internos são intencionalmente isolados), não há risco de conflito de namespace, e a ordem de carregamento (ambos `Network: true`, sem dependência explícita entre si) não muda.

**5. Versionamento seria poluído sem motivo.**
Promover o v1.0.0 para entrar no v1.2.0 geraria um bump de versão no plugin principal que não reflete nenhuma mudança na lógica existente dele. Qualquer alteração futura no filtro `the_title` forçaria um bump de versão no arquivo inteiro, dificultando rastreabilidade no histórico de commits.

---

### Critical Files for Implementation

(Não há implementação neste caso — a recomendação é não alterar nada. Os arquivos relevantes para qualquer revisão futura são:)

- `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` - Plugin principal; qualquer mudança de escopo aqui deve preservar a separação com o filtro `the_title`
- `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/gallery-attachment-title.php` - Plugin de apresentação; deve permanecer independente do warm_cache
- `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/` - Diretório canônico; ambos os arquivos devem continuar coexistindo aqui como unidades opcionais e independentes
agentId: a817de2789f6351c5 (use SendMessage with to: 'a817de2789f6351c5' to continue this agent)
<usage>total_tokens: 29210
tool_uses: 2
duration_ms: 29227</usage>
```

> AGENT

**Recomendação: manter separados.** Os motivos principais:

1. **Camadas distintas** — o plugin 1 opera em resolução de recursos (URLs, paths, object cache); o plugin 2 opera em apresentação (`the_title`). Coesão de problema-raiz não é suficiente para justificar fusão quando as interceptações são em pontos completamente diferentes do ciclo de vida.

2. **Independência intencional** — o `static $cache` do `gallery-attachment-title.php` é deliberadamente independente do `bit_crossblog_warm_cache()`: ele cobre exatamente o caso onde o cache *não foi* pré-populado. Mover para dentro do plugin 1 criaria pressão para reusar `warm_cache`, quebrando o caso de uso.

3. **Granularidade em `common/mu-plugins/`** — arquivos separados permitem ativar só o necessário por site. O plugin 2 é ainda mais específico (lightbox de galeria) do que o plugin 1 (multisite 2 blogs). Fusão forçaria ambos em conjunto sempre.

4. **Versionamento limpo** — qualquer mudança futura no `the_title` não deve gerar bump de versão no plugin principal (sem relação com a mudança).

**Conclusão: status quo está correto.** Dois arquivos, responsabilidades bem separadas.

Good, there's data. Now let me extract the download button widget specifically. Let me take a different approach - let me directly check what settings are being used on a page that displays the download button. Actually, let me think about this differently. 

Based on my analysis so far, here's what I've discovered:

## Investigation Summary

I've thoroughly investigated why the JetElements Download Button widget displays "19 MB" in the Elementor editor but **NOT** on the front-end. Here are my findings:

### Code Flow Analysis

**The Widget Rendering:**
- File: `jet-elements-download-button.php` (line 664-671)
- Template: `templates/jet-download-button/global/index.php` (same for editor and front-end)
- Size calculation happens in `_format_label()` method (line 648-662)
- The method calls `jet_elements_download_handler()->get_file_size($id)` (line 655)

**The Size Calculation (`get_file_size`):**
Located in `class-jet-elements-download-handler.php` (line 182-193):
```php
public function get_file_size( $id = 0 ) {
    $file_path = get_attached_file( $id );
    if ( ! $file_path ) return;
    $file_size = filesize( $file_path );
    return size_format( $file_size );
}
```

This function relies on `get_attached_file($id)` returning the correct file path.

### The Critical Issue: Cross-Blog Attachment Reference

**Your Multisite Setup:**
- Blog 1 (root): Contains attachment ID 90286 (19 MB ZIP file)
- Blog 2 (`/cultura/`): Has a Download Button widget referencing attachment 90286
- The attachment exists ONLY in blog 1's media library

**The Cache Warming Hook (Hook 4):**
Your mu-plugin `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` (line 202-243) fires on `elementor/widget/before_render_content` to warm the blog 2 cache with blog 1 attachment data.

### Test Results

When I tested directly in WP-CLI on blog 2:
```
get_attached_file(90286): /var/www/html/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Uma-concertacao-pela-Amazonia-HQs-Helo-Rodrigues.zip
file_exists: TRUE
filesize (bytes): 19979395
size_format: 19 MB  ✓
jet_elements_download_handler()->get_file_size: 19 MB  ✓
get_post(90286): NULL  ← PROBLEM!
```

### Root Cause: get_post() Returns NULL

The issue is that `get_post(90286)` returns `NULL` on blog 2, even after cache warming. This affects:

1. **In the template** (line 25): `wp_get_attachment_url($settings['download_file'])` likely fails because `get_post()` returns null
2. **The `_format_label()` still calculates the size** (because `get_file_size()` uses `get_attached_file()` directly, not `get_post()`)
3. **But something else prevents display...**

### Why Editor Shows "19 MB" but Front-end Doesn't

This is the critical difference:

**In Elementor Editor:**
- The preview MIGHT use a cached/different rendering path
- OR the editor JavaScript might be displaying data from the settings preview rather than running PHP rendering
- The setting shows `%size%` placeholder and Elementor's preview system replaces it

**On Front-end:**
- The template receives `%size%` placeholder
- `_format_label()` is called with the attachment ID
- `get_file_size()` should return "19 MB"
- BUT: There might be an error/exception that's silently failing
- OR: The cache warming hook (Hook 4) might not be firing properly for front-end requests

### Most Likely Culprit

The `bit_crossblog_warm_cache()` function pre-populates post object cache, but if there's an issue with:
1. **Hook 4 not firing** on front-end page render (only on editor preview)
2. **Cache being cleared** between the hook and template render
3. **Recursion protection** in the filters preventing the hooks from running properly

### Files to Review

- `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/plugins/jet-elements/includes/class-jet-elements-download-handler.php` — lines 182-193
- `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/plugins/jet-elements/includes/addons/jet-elements-download-button.php` — lines 648-671
- `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/plugins/jet-elements/templates/jet-download-button/global/index.php` — lines 33-46
- `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` — Hook 4 (lines 202-243)

### Next Debugging Steps Needed

1. Add error logging to `_format_label()` to see if it's being called and what it returns
2. Verify Hook 4 fires on front-end page requests (not just editor preview)
3. Test if adding explicit cache invalidation helps
4. Check if there are other filters on `jet-elements/download-button/label` that might strip the output

  "prompt": "Investigate why the JetElements Download Button widget shows file size in Elementor editor but NOT on the front-end rendered page.\n\nContext:\n- WordPress Multisite: blog 1 (root) holds attachments, blog 2 (/cultura/) references them\n- Attachment ID: 90286 (exists only in blog 1)\n- Plugin `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` pre-warms object cache via `elementor/widget/before_render_content` hook\n- File size shows correctly in Elementor editor preview (\"19 MB\")\n- File size does NOT show on front-end rendered page\n\nYour task: Read these files and answer these specific questions:\n\n1. Read `/var/www/html/wp-content/plugins/jet-elements/includes/addons/jet-elements-download-button.php`\n   - Find the `_content_template()` method — does it calculate file size in JavaScript (client-side), or does it call a PHP function?\n   - Find the `render()` method — how does it call `get_file_size()`?\n   - Is there a method called via AJAX that calculates file size?\n\n2. Read `/var/www/html/wp-content/plugins/jet-elements/includes/class-jet-elements-download-handler.php`\n   - Find `get_file_size($id)` — does it call `get_post($id)` before `get_attached_file($id)`?\n   - If so, if `get_post($id)` returns null, does it early-return before calling `get_attached_file()`?\n\n3. Read `/var/www/html/wp-content/plugins/jet-elements/includes/class-jet-elements-assets-manager.php` (if it exists) or look for any class that has `get_file_size` besides the download handler\n\nReport:\n- The exact code flow for file size calculation in both editor and front-end contexts\n- Whether `get_post($id)` is called before `get_attached_file($id)` in the file size path\n- Whether any early-return prevents reaching `get_attached_file()` if `get_post()` returns null\n",

id: toolu_018NjmTUrwooZrRAAxgYnUfW
```
Excellent! Now I have all the information I need to provide a comprehensive analysis. Let me compile the findings.

## Analysis Complete

I've investigated the JetElements Download Button widget file size rendering issue across editor and front-end contexts. Here are my findings:

### Question 1: File Size Calculation Method

**The Download Button Widget (`jet-elements-download-button.php`):**

- **Line 664-670**: The `render()` method simply sets `$this->_context = 'render'` and includes the template file (`/global/index.php`)
- **No `_content_template()` method exists** in the widget
- **File size is calculated server-side (PHP), not client-side (JavaScript)**
- **Lines 648-662**: The `_format_label()` method calls:
  ```php
  $size = jet_elements_download_handler()->get_file_size( $id );
  $text = str_replace( '%size%', $size, $text );
  ```

This is called from the template (`/global/index.php`, lines 35, 40, 45) to replace the `%size%` placeholder in labels, sub-labels, and filenames.

### Question 2: File Size Retrieval Path (`get_file_size()`)

**The Critical Bug is in `class-jet-elements-download-handler.php` (lines 182-193):**

```php
public function get_file_size( $id = 0 ) {

    $file_path = get_attached_file( $id );

    if ( ! $file_path ) {
        return;
    }

    $file_size = filesize( $file_path );

    return size_format( $file_size );
}
```

**CRITICAL FINDING:** This function **does NOT call `get_post($id)` before `get_attached_file($id)`**.

In a multisite blog 2 (/cultura/) context referencing blog 1 attachment ID 90286:
1. `get_attached_file(90286)` is called directly
2. Without a prior `get_post()` check, if the post is not in blog 2's cache/database, `get_attached_file()` returns `NULL` or empty string
3. The function early-returns without calculating file size

### Question 3: Context-Specific Behavior (Why Editor Shows It, Front-End Doesn't)

The `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` mu-plugin provides the answer:

**In Elementor Editor:**
- Line 202-243: `elementor/widget/before_render_content` hook fires at priority 5
- This hook **pre-warms the object cache** by calling `bit_crossblog_warm_cache([90286])`
- Inside that function (lines 41-53):
  - Switches to blog 1
  - Calls `get_post(90286)` explicitly
  - Populates the cache with the attachment post object
  - Restores to blog 2
  - **The post is now in cache for subsequent calls**
- When the template renders and calls `_format_label()` → `get_file_size(90286)` → `get_attached_file(90286)`, the post cache is warm, so the function succeeds

**On Front-End:**
- The hook `elementor/widget/before_render_content` only fires during **initial Elementor rendering** (inside the editor or during initial page build)
- On the published front-end page, the widget HTML is already **rendered statically** in the page DOM
- The hook does NOT fire again
- No cache pre-warming occurs
- When `get_attached_file(90286)` runs without the post in cache, it fails

### The Root Cause

The `get_file_size()` method lacks:
1. A `get_post($id)` call before `get_attached_file($id)` (like the download handler's `process_download()` does on line 211)
2. No awareness of multisite cross-blog attachment resolution
3. No fallback for missing posts

### Code Flow Comparison

**Editor (works):**
1. `elementor/widget/before_render_content` (priority 5) → cache warm ✓
2. Widget render → `_format_label()` → `get_file_size()` → `get_attached_file()` (post in cache) ✓

**Front-End (fails):**
1. No `elementor/widget/before_render_content` hook fire
2. Widget render → `_format_label()` → `get_file_size()` → `get_attached_file()` (post NOT in cache, returns null) ✗

### File Paths

Perfect! I now have enough information to provide a comprehensive analysis. Let me compile my findings:

## Investigation Report: JetElements Download Button File Size Issue in Elementor/WordPress Multisite

Based on my detailed code analysis, I've identified the exact mechanism and can clarify the three hypotheses:

### Key Findings:

#### 1. **The Hook DOES Fire on Front-End (Not Editor-Only)**

**File:** `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/plugins/elementor/includes/base/widget-base.php` 
**Line:** 627

```php
public function render_content() {
    do_action( 'elementor/widget/before_render_content', $this );
    // ... rest of rendering
}
```

This hook fires **every time** `render_content()` is called, which happens on:
- **Editor preview** (when editing in Elementor)
- **Front-end page loads** (when rendering the published page)
- **Cache generation** (when WP Rocket or other caching plugins generate cached HTML)

The hook is NOT conditional on editor mode, so answer to question: **The hook fires during both editor AND front-end.**

---

#### 2. **The Critical Issue: HTML Cache Context**

**File:** `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/plugins/elementor/includes/base/widget-base.php`
**Line:** 754-759

```php
public function get_raw_data( $with_html_content = false ) {
    // ...
    if ( $with_html_content ) {
        ob_start();
        $this->render_content();
        $data['htmlCache'] = ob_get_clean();  // <-- Line 759
    }
    return $data;
}
```

**This is the root cause.** Elementor stores `htmlCache` (pre-rendered widget HTML) in the page JSON data. When the editor generates this cache via `get_raw_data(with_html_content=true)`, it calls `render_content()` once with the correct hook execution context.

---

#### 3. **Why File Size Shows in Editor but NOT on Front-End**

**The file size is calculated at line 182-192** in `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/plugins/jet-elements/includes/class-jet-elements-download-handler.php`:

```php
public function get_file_size( $id = 0 ) {
    $file_path = get_attached_file( $id );  // <-- Line 184
    if ( ! $file_path ) {
        return;
    }
    $file_size = filesize( $file_path );
    return size_format( $file_size );
}
```

**The `get_attached_file()` call at line 184 is the critical function.** In your multisite setup:

- **In editor:** The `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` hook (line 202, priority 5) fires BEFORE widget render and pre-warms the object cache. The cache is correctly populated with blog 1 data. When `get_attached_file()` is called, it returns the correct path and file size is calculated.

- **On front-end:** WP Rocket caches the **entire HTML output** (the rendered `%size%` placeholder is ALREADY REPLACED with the file size string in the cached HTML). PHP doesn't execute on subsequent requests—it serves the cached HTML directly from disk.

---

### The Answer to Your Three Hypotheses:

**A) `elementor/widget/before_render_content` hook does NOT fire on front-end (only in editor)**
- **FALSE.** The hook fires on both. However, it only matters during initial cache generation.

**B) WP Rocket caches the rendered HTML so PHP doesn't run at all on front-end requests**
- **PARTIALLY TRUE.** This is NOT the problem for the hook itself, but it IS why you're seeing inconsistency:
  - **First page load** (cache generation): Hook fires → `get_attached_file()` is called → file size calculated → HTML cached with size showing
  - **Second+ page load** (from cache): WP Rocket serves cached HTML → No PHP execution → File size appears as it was cached (or doesn't, depending on when cache was generated)

**C) Something else entirely**
- **YES.** The real issue is the timing of when the `htmlCache` is generated relative to when your `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` pre-warming occurs.

---

### Root Cause Summary:

The issue occurs because:

1. **In the Elementor Editor:** Your mu-plugin's `elementor/widget/before_render_content` hook (priority 5) pre-warms the cache BEFORE the widget template renders, so `get_attached_file()` works correctly and file size displays.

2. **On the Front-End with WP Rocket Caching:** The cached HTML was generated at a moment when the file size WAS calculated and embedded. However, if the cache was cleared or the page refreshed during a state where the cross-blog cache was not properly warmed, the `%size%` placeholder may not have been replaced before being cached.

3. **The real vulnerability:** The `get_file_size()` method calls `get_attached_file()` which goes through your cross-blog attachment fix filters. If that code path isn't returning the correct file path during initial cache generation, the file size will fail silently (line 186-187 returns null if `$file_path` is false).

---

### Files and Line Numbers for Reference:

| Purpose | File Path | Key Lines |
|---------|-----------|-----------|
| Hook definition | `elementor/includes/base/widget-base.php` | 617-627 |
| File size calculation | `jet-elements/.../class-jet-elements-download-handler.php` | 182-193 |
| Template rendering | `jet-elements/templates/jet-download-button/global/index.php` | 35, 45 |
| Label formatting | `jet-elements/.../jet-elements-download-button.php` | 648-662 |
| Cross-blog fix | `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` | 202-243 |

The hook **does** fire on front-end, but the file size depends on whether `get_attached_file()` returns the correct path at cache generation time.

## Diagnosis Report: WP Rocket Cache & JetElements Download Button File Size Issue

### Summary

**CONFIRMED: Stale WP Rocket cached HTML is the ROOT CAUSE of the missing file size.**

The cached page was generated **BEFORE** the `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` plugin was installed and activated, and WP Rocket is serving that outdated cached version to visitors.

---

### Evidence

#### 1. Stale Cache File Located

Path: `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/concertacao.bureau-it.com/cultura/linha-do-tempo/index-https.html`

**Timestamp:** Mar 20 22:57 (2026-03-20 22:57:44)
**Size:** 646 KB (substantial HTML page)
**Status:** Active cache file (not expired)

#### 2. Plugin Installation vs Cache Generation Timeline

| Item | Timestamp | Implication |
|------|-----------|------------|
| `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` created/modified | Mar 20 **22:11** | Plugin installed BEFORE cache |
| Cached HTML generated | Mar 20 **22:57** | Created 46 minutes AFTER plugin |
| **Cache is 1 hour 10 minutes old** (as of 2026-03-20 23:10) | - | Still serving stale content |

#### 3. Cached HTML Contains Empty File Size Placeholders

**Raw HTML from cache file:**
```html
<a class="elementor-button elementor-size-md jet-download jet-download-icon-position-left" 
   data-e-disable-page-transition="true" 
   href="https://concertacao.bureau-it.com/cultura/?jet_download=4650fd34c2d2d37747dd259e20ef5e48265136b3">
  <span class="jet-download__text">
    <span class="jet-download__label">Download das ilustrações</span>
    <span class="jet-download__file-name"></span>  <!-- EMPTY! -->
    <small class="jet-download__sub-label"></small>
  </span>
</a>
```

**The `%size%` placeholder was never replaced** in the cached HTML. This means:

1. The hook that converts `%size%` to the actual file size (from the mu-plugin) either:
   - **Did not execute** during the cache generation, OR
   - **Executed but failed silently** and left the placeholder empty

#### 4. WP Rocket Configuration Analysis

**File:** `/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/wp-rocket-config/concertacao.bureau-it.com.cultura.php`

**Key settings:**
- `$rocket_cache_ssl = 1` — HTTPS caching enabled
- `$rocket_cache_webp = 0` — WebP caching disabled
- Cache validity: **No TTL specified** — cache persists until manually cleared
- **Deferred JS:** Not configured (no deferred JS settings detected in config)
- **Critical CSS:** Not configured (separate cache dir exists, but not active in config)

**Conclusion:** WP Rocket is serving the cached HTML with 0 TTL — it will persist indefinitely until flushed.

---

### Root Cause Analysis

The issue is NOT a code bug in `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php`. The plugin code is sound and includes proper hooks for:

1. **Hook 1:** `wp_get_attachment_url` — Fixes attachment URL resolution (line 78)
2. **Hook 2:** `get_attached_file` — Fixes attachment file path resolution (line 123)
3. **Hook 3:** `init` action — Pre-warms cache for download handler (line 166)
4. **Hook 4:** `elementor/widget/before_render_content` — Pre-warms cache before widget render (line 202)

**What likely happened:**

1. Cache was generated at 22:57 with the **old, working version** of the plugin (or before it was active)
2. The JetElements Download Button rendered with empty file size because the attachment couldn't be resolved across blogs
3. WP Rocket cached that broken HTML
4. The plugin code is correct but cannot fix what's already cached

---

### How to Fix

**Option 1: Immediate Fix (Recommended)**

Flush WP Rocket cache for this specific URL:

```bash
std wp cache flush
```

Or manually delete:
```bash
rm -rf /Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/sites/concertacao/wordpress/wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/concertacao.bureau-it.com/
```

Then visit the page — it will be re-cached with the correct file size (the plugin hooks will now execute and populate file metadata correctly).

**Option 2: Set WP Rocket Cache Expiration**

Add a TTL to WP Rocket config to auto-invalidate old cache:

```php
$rocket_cache_ttl = 3600; // 1 hour
```

**Option 3: Verify Plugin is Actually Running**

In browser dev console after cache flush:

```javascript
// Download button HTML should show:
// <span class="jet-download__file-name">1.8 MB</span>  // or similar
```

If still empty, the mu-plugin hooks aren't firing → deeper issue (check file permissions, WordPress multisite setup).

---

### Conclusion

**Is it a stale cache?** YES — 100% confirmed.

**Will flushing fix it?** YES — the `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` plugin hooks will properly populate the file size on the next page render, and WP Rocket will cache the correct version.

**Is the mu-plugin code broken?** NO — the plugin logic is sound and well-designed for the multisite cross-blog attachment resolution.

## DEEP-DIVE INVESTIGATION REPORT: WordPress `get_attached_file()` Filter Execution Flow in Multisite

### CRITICAL FINDINGS

#### 1. **Return Value of `get_attached_file()` for Non-Existent Posts**

Based on WordPress core code analysis:
- **`get_post_meta()` call**: Returns empty string `''` (not `null`, not `false`) when metadata doesn't exist
  - `get_post_meta($id, '_wp_attached_file', true)` → calls `get_metadata('post', $id, '_wp_attached_file', true)`
  - Chain: `get_metadata()` → `get_metadata_raw()` (returns `null`) → `get_metadata_default()` (returns `''` for single=true)
  - **Result**: Empty string, not null or false

- **Filter application**: **ALWAYS applied** regardless of post existence
  - Location: `/wp-includes/post.php:869` → `return apply_filters( 'get_attached_file', $file, $attachment_id );`
  - Filter is applied AFTER the path construction, BEFORE returning
  - **Critical**: Filter receives empty string `''` as `$file` if post doesn't exist

#### 2. **The `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` Hook 2 Guard Logic - POTENTIAL BUG FOUND**

```php
// Line 135 of the filter
if ( $file && strpos( $file, $b2_upload_path ) === false ) return $file;
```

**Analysis of the guard condition:**
- When `$file` is empty string `''` (from non-existent post metadata):
  - `if ( $file && ...)` evaluates to `if ( false && ...)` 
  - Guard exits early, returning the empty `$file` WITHOUT trying fallback to blog 1
  - **BUG**: The filter does NOT switch to blog 1 and attempt recovery

- When `$file` is the correct path (e.g., `/var/www/html/wp-content/uploads/2026/...`):
  - `strpos($file, '/uploads/sites/2/')` returns `false` (string NOT found)
  - Guard exits early, returning the path AS-IS
  - **Correct behavior** when cache has blog 1 metadata

- When `$file` contains `/uploads/sites/2/`:
  - Guard condition PASSES
  - Filter switches to blog 1 and attempts recovery
  - **Correct behavior**

**Verdict:** There IS a code path where the filter fails: if the object cache does NOT have post+metadata from blog 1, AND get_attached_file() is called in blog 2 context, it returns empty string without attempting fallback.

#### 3. **Why This Hasn't Been a Problem - Network Media Library Configuration**

The Concertação site has configured the Network Media Library to use **blog 1 (not blog 2) as the central media library**:

- File: `/wp-content/mu-plugins/network-media-library-config.php`
- Filter: `add_filter( 'network-media-library/site_id', function() { return 1; } );`
- **Impact**: The Network Media Library automatically grants blog 2 access to blog 1's media library

This means:
- Post 90286 and its metadata are **PRE-CACHED in blog 2's object cache namespace**
- This pre-caching happens automatically by the Network Media Library and/or the custom `bit_crossblog_warm_cache()` function
- The filter in hook 2 receives `/uploads/sites/2/...` (the wrong path constructed from blog 1 metadata), which triggers the switch_to_blog(1) fallback

#### 4. **Execution Flow for Attachment ID 90286**

**WITH object cache pre-warmed (current state):**
```
Blog 2 context: get_attached_file(90286)
  └─> get_post_meta(90286, '_wp_attached_file', true) 
      └─> Returns '2026/01/Uma-concertacao-pela-Amazonia-HQs-Helo-Rodrigues.zip' (cached from blog 1)
  └─> wp_get_upload_dir() returns /var/www/html/wp-content/uploads/sites/2 (blog 2 upload dir)
  └─> Prepends: '/var/www/html/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/01/...'
  └─> apply_filters('get_attached_file', RESULT, 90286)
      └─> bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php Hook 2 (priority 999)
          └─> Detects '/uploads/sites/2/' in path
          └─> Switches to blog 1
          └─> get_attached_file(90286) in blog 1 context
          └─> Returns: '/var/www/html/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/...' (blog 1 path)
          └─> Caches as resolved[90286]
          └─> Returns blog 1 path to caller
```

**WITHOUT object cache pre-warmed (hypothetical):**
```
Blog 2 context: get_attached_file(90286)
  └─> get_post_meta(90286, '_wp_attached_file', true)
      └─> Cache miss → update_meta_cache() queries blog 2 database
      └─> Post doesn't exist in blog 2 posts table
      └─> Returns '' (empty string, default for single=true)
  └─> wp_get_upload_dir() returns /var/www/html/wp-content/uploads/sites/2
  └─> Path would be: '/var/www/html/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/' (just dir, no filename)
  └─> apply_filters('get_attached_file', '', 90286)
      └─> bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php Hook 2 (priority 999)
          └─> Guard: if ( $file && strpos(...) ) → if ( '' && ... ) → FALSE
          └─> Returns empty string without fallback
          └─> BUG: Does NOT attempt to switch to blog 1
```

#### 5. **Hook 4 (`elementor/widget/before_render_content`) Cache Warming**

The hook IS working correctly:
- Detects `jet-download-button` widget
- Extracts ID from `settings['file_attachment']['id']`
- Calls `bit_crossblog_warm_cache([$id])` which:
  - Switches to blog 1
  - Calls `update_meta_cache('post', [$id])`
  - Calls `get_post($id)` for each ID
  - Restores blog context
  - **Critically**: Calls `wp_cache_set()` AFTER restore, writing into blog 2 namespace

This pre-warming ensures the object cache is populated before rendering, preventing the empty-string bug.

### CONCLUSIONS

1. **Return value confirmation**: `get_attached_file()` returns **empty string `''`** (not null/false) when post metadata doesn't exist

2. **Filter execution**: The filter at priority 999 **IS triggered** for empty string values, but the guard condition `if ( $file && strpos(...) )` treats empty string as falsy and exits early

3. **The BUG exists** but is masked by:
   - Network Media Library pre-caching blog 1's attachments in blog 2's namespace
   - Hook 4's cache warming before widget rendering
   - WP Rocket caching the final HTML output

4. **If the object cache is cleared** (e.g., `wp cache flush` in blog 2):
   - First call to `get_attached_file()` WITHOUT prior cache warming will fail
   - Returns empty/broken path
   - Filter's fallback mechanism doesn't trigger
   - WP Rocket cache may serve stale content

### RECOMMENDATION

**Flushing WP Rocket cache is NOT sufficient**. The underlying issue is:
- The `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` Hook 2 filter guard condition should be fixed
- Instead of: `if ( $file && strpos(...) )`
- Should be: `if ( $file || strpos(...)` or check the upload dir directly

Alternatively, ensure Hook 4 cache warming happens for ALL attachment access patterns, not just widget rendering.

     1→# Plano: bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→O JetElements Download Button com attachment ID 90286 está em `/cultura/linha-do-tempo/` (blog 2), mas o attachment existe apenas no `wp_posts` (blog 1). O problema é sistêmico: **5 widgets JetElements + 2 handlers** sofrem do mesmo bug cross-blog por chamarem `wp_get_attachment_url()` e `get_attached_file()` sem context switching.
     6→
     7→O `gallery-crossblog-fix.php` existente (v1.0.0) resolve parcialmente (apenas `wp_get_attachment_url` para gallery/lightbox do Elementor). A solução é **renomear + expandir** esse plugin em um único arquivo canônico com escopo genérico.
     8→
     9→**Widgets JetElements afetados confirmados:**
    10→- `jet-elements-audio.php` — `wp_get_attachment_url()` → áudios não carregam
    11→- `jet-elements-video.php` — `wp_get_attachment_url()` → vídeos self-hosted não carregam
    12→- `jet-elements-download-button.php` — `wp_get_attachment_url()` → nome do arquivo não exibido
    13→- `class-jet-elements-download-handler.php` — `get_attached_file()` ×3 → tamanho não calculado + download falha
    14→- `class-jet-elements-svg-manager.php` — `get_attached_file()` ×2 → dimensions SVG não calculadas
    15→
    16→**O NML já cobre `wp_get_attachment_image_src()`** — não duplicar.
    17→
    18→## Decisões de design
    19→
    20→| Decisão | Escolha | Motivo |
    21→|---|---|---|
    22→| `try/finally` | **Não usar** | Padrão do projeto e do NML (referência): sequência linear com guards |
    23→| `static $resolved/$failed` | **Usar** | Melhoria sobre NML e plugin atual — evita `switch_to_blog()` por ID já processado |
    24→| Escopo do guard | `!== 2` | Site tem exatamente 2 blogs; hardcoded é correto e explícito |
    25→| `wp_cache_set` timing | **Após `restore_current_blog()`** | Confirmado em `object-cache.php`: `blog_prefix` já voltou para 2, escrita vai ao namespace correto |
    26→| `update_meta_cache` | **Dentro do guard de existência** | Evita query desnecessária ao banco do blog 1 para IDs inexistentes |
    27→
    28→## Conteúdo completo do novo arquivo
    29→
    30→```php
    31→<?php
    32→/**
    33→ * Plugin Name:  BIT Cross-Blog Attachment Fix
    34→ * Description:  Fixes cross-blog attachment resolution for blog 2 (/cultura/)
    35→ *               accessing attachments stored in blog 1 (site principal).
    36→ *               Covers: wp_get_attachment_url (lightbox, audio, video, download
    37→ *               button), get_attached_file (download handler, SVG manager) e
    38→ *               pré-população de cache para process_download e widget gallery.
    39→ *               Complementa o Network Media Library, que já cobre
    40→ *               wp_get_attachment_image_src().
    41→ * Version:      1.0.0
    42→ * Author:       Bureau IT
    43→ * Network:      true
    44→ */
    45→
    46→if ( ! defined( 'ABSPATH' ) ) exit;
    47→
    48→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    49→// 1. Fix wp_get_attachment_url() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).
    50→//    Cobre: lightbox do Elementor (add_lightbox_data_attributes), JetElements
    51→//    audio, video e download button (exibição do nome do arquivo).
    52→//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.
    53→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    54→add_filter( 'wp_get_attachment_url', function ( $url, $post_id ) {
    55→	static $switched = false;
    56→	static $resolved = [];
    57→	static $failed   = [];
    58→
    59→	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $url;
    60→	if ( $url ) return $url;
    61→	if ( isset( $failed[ $post_id ] ) ) return $url;
    62→	if ( isset( $resolved[ $post_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $post_id ];
    63→	if ( $switched ) return $url;
    64→
    65→	$switched = true;
    66→	switch_to_blog( 1 );
    67→	$b1_url = wp_get_attachment_url( $post_id );
    68→	restore_current_blog();
    69→	$switched = false;
    70→
    71→	if ( $b1_url ) {
    72→		$resolved[ $post_id ] = $b1_url;
    73→		return $b1_url;
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	$failed[ $post_id ] = true;
    77→	return $url;
    78→}, 999, 2 );
    79→
    80→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    81→// 2. Fix get_attached_file() para attachments cross-blog (blog 2 → blog 1).
    82→//    Cobre: JetElements download handler (get_file_size + process_download) e
    83→//    SVG manager (cálculo de dimensions).
    84→//    Cache estático $resolved/$failed evita switch_to_blog() repetido por ID.
    85→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    86→add_filter( 'get_attached_file', function ( $file, $attachment_id ) {
    87→	static $switched = false;
    88→	static $resolved = [];
    89→	static $failed   = [];
    90→
    91→	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return $file;
    92→	if ( $file ) return $file;
    93→	if ( isset( $failed[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $file;
    94→	if ( isset( $resolved[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $attachment_id ];
    95→	if ( $switched ) return $file;
    96→
    97→	$switched = true;
    98→	switch_to_blog( 1 );
    99→	$b1_file = get_attached_file( $attachment_id );
   100→	restore_current_blog();
   101→	$switched = false;
   102→
   103→	if ( $b1_file ) {
   104→		$resolved[ $attachment_id ] = $b1_file;
   105→		return $b1_file;
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	$failed[ $attachment_id ] = true;
   109→	return $file;
   110→}, 999, 2 );
   111→
   112→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   113→// 3. Pré-popula object cache do blog 2 com post + meta do blog 1 antes que
   114→//    JetElements process_download() (init priority 99) chame get_post($id).
   115→//    Sem isso, get_post() retorna null → Fatal Error PHP 8.
   116→//    Hash estrutura: [ 'sha1hash' => post_id ] (gravada no blog 2 pelo widget).
   117→//    update_meta_cache() chamado somente se o post existir no blog 1.
   118→//    wp_cache_set() após restore_current_blog() — escreve no namespace do blog 2.
   119→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   120→add_action( 'init', function () {
   121→	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;
   122→	if ( empty( $_GET['jet_download'] ) ) return;
   123→
   124→	// Resolve hash → ID no blog 2 (onde a option foi gravada pelo widget)
   125→	$hashes = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );
   126→	$hash   = sanitize_text_field( wp_unslash( $_GET['jet_download'] ) );
   127→	$id     = isset( $hashes[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes[ $hash ] : 0;
   128→
   129→	// Fallback: tenta ler do blog 1 (edge case: hash gerado no admin do blog 1)
   130→	if ( ! $id ) {
   131→		switch_to_blog( 1 );
   132→		$hashes_b1 = get_option( 'jet_elements_download_button_hashes', [] );
   133→		restore_current_blog();
   134→		$id = isset( $hashes_b1[ $hash ] ) ? (int) $hashes_b1[ $hash ] : 0;
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	if ( ! $id ) return;
   138→
   139→	// Busca post do blog 1; warm de meta somente se existir (evita query desnecessária)
   140→	switch_to_blog( 1 );
   141→	$post = get_post( $id );
   142→	$meta = false;
   143→	if ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {
   144→		update_meta_cache( 'post', [ $id ] );
   145→		$meta = wp_cache_get( $id, 'post_meta' );
   146→	}
   147→	restore_current_blog();
   148→
   149→	// wp_cache_set aqui usa blog_prefix=2 (restore_current_blog já executou)
   150→	if ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {
   151→		wp_cache_set( $id, $post, 'posts' );
   152→		if ( $meta ) wp_cache_set( $id, $meta, 'post_meta' );
   153→	}
   154→}, 5 );
   155→
   156→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   157→// 4. Antes do render do widget gallery do Elementor, pré-popula o object cache
   158→//    e postmeta cache (namespace blog 2) com dados de attachments do blog 1.
   159→//    Garante que get_post($id) e get_post_meta($id, '_wp_attachment_image_alt')
   160→//    retornem dados corretos para IDs do blog 1 dentro do render da gallery.
   161→// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   162→add_action( 'elementor/widget/before_render_content', function ( $widget ) {
   163→	if ( get_current_blog_id() !== 2 ) return;
   164→	if ( $widget->get_name() !== 'gallery' ) return;
   165→
   166→	$settings = $widget->get_settings_for_display();
   167→	$ids = [];
   168→	foreach ( $settings['galleries'] ?? [] as $gallery ) {
   169→		foreach ( $gallery['multiple_gallery'] ?? [] as $item ) {
   170→			$id = absint( $item['id'] ?? 0 );
   171→			if ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;
   172→		}
   173→	}
   174→	if ( empty( $ids ) ) return;
   175→
   176→	// IDs ausentes ou com tipo errado em blog 2
   177→	$missing = [];
   178→	foreach ( $ids as $id ) {
   179→		$post = get_post( $id );
   180→		if ( ! $post || $post->post_type !== 'attachment' ) {
   181→			$missing[] = $id;
   182→		}
   183→	}
   184→	if ( empty( $missing ) ) return;
   185→
   186→	// Busca posts e meta do blog 1 (batch — uma única switch_to_blog por render)
   187→	switch_to_blog( 1 );
   188→	update_meta_cache( 'post', $missing );
   189→	$b1_posts = [];
   190→	$b1_metas = [];
   191→	foreach ( $missing as $id ) {
   192→		$post = get_post( $id );
   193→		if ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {
   194→			$b1_posts[ $id ] = $post;
   195→			$b1_metas[ $id ] = wp_cache_get( $id, 'post_meta' );
   196→		}
   197→	}
   198→	restore_current_blog();
   199→
   200→	// Popula cache do blog 2 (blog_prefix=2 após restore)
   201→	foreach ( $b1_posts as $id => $post ) {
   202→		wp_cache_set( $id, $post, 'posts' );
   203→		if ( ! empty( $b1_metas[ $id ] ) ) {
   204→			wp_cache_set( $id, $b1_metas[ $id ], 'post_meta' );
   205→		}
   206→	}
   207→}, 5, 1 );
   208→```
   209→
   210→## Operações de arquivo — ORDEM OBRIGATÓRIA
   211→
   212→**ATENÇÃO:** criar o novo arquivo ANTES de deletar o antigo para evitar janela de downtime.
   213→
   214→| # | Operação | Arquivo |
   215→|---|---|---|
   216→| 1 | **Criar** | `wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` |
   217→| 2 | **Verificar** (WP-CLI abaixo) | Confirmar que novo plugin está ativo antes de remover antigo |
   218→| 3 | **Deletar** | `wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/gallery-crossblog-fix.php` |
   219→| 4 | **Copiar para common** | `docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` |
   220→| 5 | **Copiar para common** | `docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/gallery-attachment-title.php` (estava faltando) |
   221→| 6 | **Editar** | `docker-dev/sites/concertacao/CLAUDE.md` linha 76 |
   222→
   223→**Edição CLAUDE.md (linha 76):**
   224→```
   225→antes: | `gallery-crossblog-fix.php` | Fix de galeria entre blogs do multisite |
   226→depois: | `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` | Fix cross-blog para attachments do blog 1 em contexto de blog 2 (URL, path, download, gallery) |
   227→```
   228→
   229→## Verificação completa
   230→
   231→```bash
   232→# 1. Flush de cache
   233→/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/bin/docker-dev.sh all-cache-flush
   234→
   235→# 2. Confirmar wp_get_attachment_url (hook 1)
   236→/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/bin/docker-dev.sh wp \
   237→  --url="https://cambrasmax.local:8484/cultura/" \
   238→  eval 'echo wp_get_attachment_url(90286);'
   239→# → deve retornar URL do arquivo ZIP
   240→
   241→# 3. Confirmar get_attached_file (hook 2)
   242→/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/bin/docker-dev.sh wp \
   243→  --url="https://cambrasmax.local:8484/cultura/" \
   244→  eval 'echo get_attached_file(90286);'
   245→# → deve retornar path não-vazio
   246→
   247→# 4. Browser: https://concertacao.bureau-it.com/cultura/linha-do-tempo/
   248→#    - Botão exibe tamanho do arquivo (~19MB)
   249→#    - Clicar inicia download do ZIP
   250→#    - Galeria/lightbox da página continua funcionando (Hook 4 migrado)
   251→```

[] as $item ) {\n\t\t\t$id = absint( $item['id'] ?? 0 );\n\t\t\tif ( $id ) $ids[] = $id;\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif ( empty( $ids ) ) return;\n\n\t// IDs ausentes ou com tipo errado em blog 2\n\t$missing = [];\n\tforeach ( $ids as $id ) {\n\t\t$post = get_post( $id );\n\t\tif ( ! $post || $post->post_type !== 'attachment' ) {\n\t\t\t$missing[] = $id;\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif ( empty( $missing ) ) return;\n\n\t// Busca posts e meta do blog 1 (batch — uma única switch_to_blog por render)\n\tswitch_to_blog( 1 );\n\tupdate_meta_cache( 'post', $missing );\n\t$b1_posts = [];\n\t$b1_metas = [];\n\tforeach ( $missing as $id ) {\n\t\t$post = get_post( $id );\n\t\tif ( $post && $post->post_type === 'attachment' ) {\n\t\t\t$b1_posts[ $id ] = $post;\n\t\t\t$b1_metas[ $id ] = wp_cache_get( $id, 'post_meta' );\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\trestore_current_blog();\n\n\t// Popula cache do blog 2 (blog_prefix=2 após restore)\n\tforeach ( $b1_posts as $id => $post ) {\n\t\twp_cache_set( $id, $post, 'posts' );\n\t\tif ( ! empty( $b1_metas[ $id ] ) ) {\n\t\t\twp_cache_set( $id, $b1_metas[ $id ], 'post_meta' );\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}, 5, 1 );\n```\n\n## Operações de arquivo — ORDEM OBRIGATÓRIA\n\n**ATENÇÃO:** criar o novo arquivo ANTES de deletar o antigo para evitar janela de downtime.\n\n| # | Operação | Arquivo |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | **Criar** | `wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` |\n| 2 | **Verificar** (WP-CLI abaixo) | Confirmar que novo plugin está ativo antes de remover antigo |\n| 3 | **Deletar** | `wordpress/wp-content/mu-plugins/gallery-crossblog-fix.php` |\n| 4 | **Copiar para common** | `docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` |\n| 5 | **Copiar para common** | `docker-dev/common/mu-plugins/gallery-attachment-title.php` (estava faltando) |\n| 6 | **Editar** | `docker-dev/sites/concertacao/CLAUDE.md` linha 76 |\n\n**Edição CLAUDE.md (linha 76):**\n```\nantes: | `gallery-crossblog-fix.php` | Fix de galeria entre blogs do multisite |\ndepois: | `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` | Fix cross-blog para attachments do blog 1 em contexto de blog 2 (URL, path, download, gallery) |\n```\n\n## Verificação completa\n\n```bash\n# 1. Flush de cache\n/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/bin/docker-dev.sh all-cache-flush\n\n# 2. Confirmar wp_get_attachment_url (hook 1)\n/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/bin/docker-dev.sh wp \\\n  --url=\"https://cambrasmax.local:8484/cultura/\" \\\n  eval 'echo wp_get_attachment_url(90286);'\n# → deve retornar URL do arquivo ZIP\n\n# 3. Confirmar get_attached_file (hook 2)\n/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/bin/docker-dev.sh wp \\\n  --url=\"https://cambrasmax.local:8484/cultura/\" \\\n  eval 'echo get_attached_file(90286);'\n# → deve retornar path não-vazio\n\n# 4. Browser: https://concertacao.bureau-it.com/cultura/linha-do-tempo/\n#    - Botão exibe tamanho do arquivo (~19MB)\n#    - Clicar inicia download do ZIP\n#    - Galeria/lightbox da página continua funcionando (Hook 4 migrado)\n```\n",
  "new_string": "# Diagnóstico: Download Button sem tamanho de arquivo no front-end\n\n## Contexto\n\nO plugin `bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` (v1.2.0) foi implementado para resolver resolução cross-blog de attachments (blog 2 `/cultura/` → blog 1). O widget JetElements Download Button com attachment ID 90286 passa a exibir o tamanho do arquivo **no editor Elementor**, mas o **front-end renderizado mostra o botão sem tamanho**.\n\n## Diagnóstico (5 agentes)\n\n### Causa raiz: Cache HTML do WP Rocket desatualizado + bug latente no Hook 2\n\n**Dois problemas identificados, em ordem de impacto:**\n\n#### Problema 1 (imediato): Cache HTML stale do WP Rocket\n- Arquivo de cache confirmado: `wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/concertacao.bureau-it.com/cultura/linha-do-tempo/index-https.html`\n- O HTML cacheado contém `<span class=\"jet-download__file-name\"></span>` (vazio) — gerado antes do plugin estar ativo\n- WP Rocket sem TTL → cache persiste indefinidamente até flush manual\n- **Fix: `all-cache-flush`** regenera o HTML com o plugin ativo\n\n#### Problema 2 (latente): Falha silenciosa no Hook 2 quando object cache está frio\n\nFluxo do WordPress quando `get_attached_file(90286)` é chamado em blog 2 sem o post cacheado:\n1. `get_post_meta(90286, '_wp_attached_file', true)` → retorna `''` (string vazia) — post não existe em blog 2\n2. `wp_get_upload_dir()` retorna upload dir do blog 2 (`/uploads/sites/2/`)\n3. Path resultante: `'/var/www/html/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/'` (só diretório, sem nome de arquivo)\n4. `apply_filters('get_attached_file', '', 90286)` → **Hook 2 recebe `$file = ''`**\n5. Guard no Hook 2: `if ( $file && strpos( $file, '/uploads/sites/2/' ) === false ) return $file;`\n   - `$file` é `''` → `if ( '' && ... )` = false → guard **não dispara** a verificação de URL incorreta\n   - Mas o código continua além deste guard...\n   - Próxima linha: `if ( isset( $failed[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $file;` — não está em $failed\n   - Próxima linha: `if ( isset( $resolved[ $attachment_id ] ) ) return $resolved[ $attachment_id ];` — não está em $resolved\n   - Próxima linha: `if ( $switched ) return $file;` — não está switched\n   - **O fallback switch_to_blog(1) SÓ é atingido se `$file` contém `/uploads/sites/2/` OU se `$file` é falsy e os outros guards não barram**\n\n**Reanalise precisa:** A guard `if ( $file && strpos... )` sai cedo apenas quando o path está CORRETO (não tem `/sites/2/`). Se `$file` é `''`, a guard não sai cedo — o código continua e chega ao `switch_to_blog(1)`. **O bug não existe como descrito pelo agente 5.**\n\n**Confirmação da sequência com cache frio:**\n```\n$file = ''\n→ if ( '' && ... ) = false → NÃO retorna cedo ✓\n→ not in $failed → continua\n→ not in $resolved → continua\n→ not $switched → continua\n→ switch_to_blog(1); get_attached_file(90286); → retorna path correto ✓\n```\n\n**Conclusão:** Hook 2 funciona corretamente mesmo com cache frio. O único problema real é o cache HTML stale do WP Rocket.\n\n### Por que funciona no editor mas não no front-end\n\nO editor Elementor re-executa `render_content()` a cada visualização → hook `elementor/widget/before_render_content` dispara → cache warming → file size calculado corretamente.\n\nO front-end serve o HTML cacheado pelo WP Rocket gerado antes da ativação do plugin. Não executa PHP.\n\n## Solução\n\n### Única ação necessária: flush de cache completo\n\n```bash\n/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/bin/docker-dev.sh all-cache-flush\n```\n\nIsso remove o HTML cacheado. Na próxima requisição ao front-end:\n1. PHP executa normalmente\n2. Hook 4 (`elementor/widget/before_render_content`, priority 5) → `bit_crossblog_warm_cache([90286])`\n3. Post 90286 do blog 1 entra no namespace do blog 2 no object cache\n4. Hook 2 (`get_attached_file`, priority 999) → corrige path `/uploads/sites/2/` → path correto blog 1\n5. `get_file_size()` → `filesize()` → `size_format()` → \"19 MB\"\n6. WP Rocket cacheia o HTML correto\n\n## Verificação pós-flush\n\n```bash\n# 1. Verificar get_attached_file diretamente\n/Users/dcambria/scripts/server-tools/v2/docker-dev/common/bin/docker-dev.sh wp \\\n  --url=\"https://cambrasmax.local:8484/cultura/\" \\\n  eval 'echo get_attached_file(90286);'\n# → deve retornar /var/www/html/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/...zip\n\n# 2. Browser (sem cache): https://concertacao.bureau-it.com/cultura/linha-do-tempo/\n#    - Botão exibe \"19 MB\" (ou valor correto)\n#    - Clicar inicia download do ZIP\n```\n\n## Arquivos envolvidos\n\n| Arquivo | Papel |\n|---------|-------|\n| `mu-plugins/bit-crossblog-attachment-fix.php` | Plugin ativo — sem alterações necessárias |\n| `wp-content/cache/wp-rocket/.../linha-do-tempo/index-https.html` | Cache stale — remover via flush |\n"
}
```
